Programs](../sources/S-SGLANG.md)：固定版本 2312.07104v2 的 §3 与附录 A 用 RadixAttention 保存前缀 KV，以活跃引用保护、LRU 叶优先和缓存感知调度处理复用。提供摘录中的实例采用单 token 页，不能把树节点等同于本章固定块。附录 A.4 的数据并行 meta-tree [全文文本](../../references/S-SGLANG/fulltext.txt)\n\n本轮按提供的完整正文、方法、审查标准、来源说明及明确标注的论文摘录核对；摘录不代表阅读全文，也未读取上游源码。链式身份的下标约定、共同位置与清单取得协议、租约/代次及发布回收边界、资源账、设定轨迹和区分实验为本库基于固定材料的推导，不归称为论文原样算法或 InfraAgents 设备实测。来源版本上限为 2026-04-30；编辑日期不改变来源时间。\n\n## 延伸阅读\n\n- [前缀缓存：计算等价类、Radix 与淘汰收益](../algorithms/prefix-cache-identity.md)。\n- [多级驻留：有效副本、预取、淘汰与重算](../algorithms/tiered-residency.md)。\n- [KV "833367b4ebb988bc089a1dbb73b61d7e5dffaff0d4c8622c68c9e33ddbd77560", "reading_scope": "excerpt", "category": "related_chapter", "content": "[EXCERPT extracted-character range 0:10045]\n# K-ALG-023：请求路由与迁移：命中收益、排队和追赶条件\n\n路由选择请求去哪里执行；迁移允许在请求实际状态和负载变化后重新选择。它们的动机来自三个相互牵制的量：缓存亲和可以减少重算，分散执行可以减少排队和干扰，集中空闲容量又可能帮助长请求准入。选择必须比较同一请求、同一任务终点的完整路径，并保持输入、模型状态、执行授权与输出提交一致。\n\n## 请求状态与候选计划\n\n固定计算身份 η，包括模型与权重、adapter、规范化输入、外部特征、位置和窗口规则、数值合同及隔离命名空间。表示合同另说明 dtype、量化尺度、层与 head 几何、布局、分页和分片。相同 token、shape 或地址不足以证明状态可互换。\n\n请求的已知输入为 x[0:A)，已消费位置为 p，S(p) 表示所有必需状态族共同完成前 p 个输入后的状态。下一次消费位置为 p；已采样而未消费的 token 可以已在 x[p:A) 中，但其 KV 尚不存在。普通全注意力模型的全层 KV [0:p) 也不自动包含末行 logits、采样状态或输出游标。\n\n| 对象 | 含义与责任 |\n|---|---|\n| R、branch | 逻辑请求及保留输出分支，贯穿执行地点变化 |\n| g、e | 执行尝试和权威授权代次；旧 e 不能再提交推进或输出 |\n| tx | 一次迁移尝试，绑定源/目标实例 incarnation、η 和阶段计划 |\n| p、A、ticket | 已消费边界、已知输入终点，以及必要 logits/未消费 token 的位置与消费记录 |\n| manifest | 恢复所需状态族、层、范围、表示、分片和目标分配绑定的完整清单 |\n| lease | 源读取保护或目标容量承诺；各自绑定对象、代次和责任范围 |\n| READY、PREPARED | 数据完整可见；其中 PREPARED 仍等待业务接管授权 |\n| operation ledger | 已接受或接受未知的读写及其完成、最后使用和排空责任 |\n\n路由输出为 ADMIT(i,plan,lease)、有界 WAIT 或 REJECT。ADMIT 承诺的是合法执行计划，不能代替模型执行完成。迁移输出为目标接管、确定中止，或保留恢复与排空责任的未决状态。\n\n全过程保持以下不变量：身份和共同恢复位置相容；读取期间源内容稳定；接收与增长之前已有足够容量；完整 manifest 和消费者可见性成立后才执行；同一请求只有一个有效提交权；最后实际访问结束前不复用存储。取消、超时、lease 过期和旧结果被拒绝，都不证明 DMA、kernel 或网络访问已经结束。\n\n## 合法集合与完成时间\n\n候选不是一个实例编号，而是实例与恢复、重算、传输方案的组合。同一实例可以具有多个合法计划，例如使用较短本地前缀，或从远端取得较长前缀。先求合法集合，再比较成本。\n\n实际恢复位置 c 必须属于全部必需状态族的共同恢复集合，并满足输入与表示合同。不能仅取各状态族最大位置的最小值；集合可能有空洞。固定完整块缓存时，未满尾块不进入共享目录，但迁移可以传明确有效的非整块尾部。\n\nc=0 可以是合法冷路径：按模型初始化规则从输入重算，此时不要求存在非空缓存副本。c>0 的复用计划则须验证副本 READY、身份和完整范围，并取得源读取保护。目录存在只形成候选。完整 KV 命中但没有末行 logits 或 token 票据时，也必须安排合法后继：普通 causal decoder 可以取得 [0,A−1) 历史并在私有写区重算最后一个已知 token；递归模型须从真实共同检查点重放，不能只修改长度。\n\n容量需求包含新增私有状态、共享尾页 COW、接收与转换暂存、页表和元数据、增长承诺及输出缓冲。对共享物理对象求并集；预留池与其中对象不重复相加。已取消但未排空的页面不能作为当前空闲量。滚动预留是一种选择，每次增长前都要重新检查，并定义不足时的等待、换出、重算或终止路线。\n\n固定 now 和任务终点，串行基线的剩余时间为\n\nC_i=Tq_i+Tprep_i+Tmiss_i+Tfetch_i+Texec_i+Thandoff_i，F_i=now+C_i。\n\nTq 是排队，Tprep 是查找、保护、预留和描述准备；Tmiss 是未命中输入或恢复重放；Tfetch 是实际进入路径的状态取得与转换；Texec 是到所选终点的剩余执行；Thandoff 是接管和必要输出交付。各项范围必须互斥，不能把同一次末行重算同时计入 Tmiss 和 Texec。后缀仍读取长历史，不能按命中 token 比例直接扣减整个前向时间。\n\n存在重叠时，沿依赖图计算事件完成时间 f(v)=d(v)+max f(u)，其中 u 遍历 v 的前驱；共享设备或链路的串行占用也须形成约束。不能把全部 profiler 区间相加，也不能无条件取最大值。绝对截止时间 d 要与 F_i 比较；剩余预算为 d−now，等待后重新计算。\n\n设定 A 有本地前缀，排队 100 ms、剩余计算 10 ms；B 从零重算 80 ms、无排队；C 排队 10 ms、远端取得状态 40 ms、剩余计算 10 ms。其他项设为零且串行，成本为 110、80、60 ms，应选 C。若 C 容量不足或其复用源未 READY，改选 B；若三个候选的 η 都与请求不符，则应全部拒绝，而非放宽身份检查。\n\n最短请求队列、最少 token、最低内存占用和最短完成时间不等价。输出长度未知时可以使用带误差范围的延迟预测，但预测不能授权越界分配，也不能把估计达标写成真实 SLO 保证。租户份额、等待上限和拒绝限制按服务合同定义，不由总体 goodput 自动满足。\n\n## 从快照到实际准入\n\n[Mooncake](../sources/S-MOONCAKE.md) §5–6 将缓存局部性、负载与 SLO 联合考虑；[Llumnix](../sources/S-LLUMNIX.md) §4 将全局实例配对与本地请求选择分开。这些组织减少全局跟踪成本，但全局报告仍是形成候选的快照，不能直接成为执行许可。\n\n```pseudocode\n算法 ROUTE(R, snapshot, contract, wait_deadline)\n输入：请求、带实例代次与版本的快照、服务合同、固定等待终点\n输出：ADMIT(i,plan,lease)、WAIT(until,reason) 或 REJECT(reason)\n\n1 验证请求身份、输入、长度、输出预算和租户资格；终态请求不再准入\n2 固定本次 now、任务终点、绝对截止时间和有限重试预算\n3 plans ← 空\n4 对每个实例 i 及其有限恢复/重算方案：\n5     核对实例 incarnation、接受状态、执行组、η 和输出路径\n6     核对共同恢复位置 c、表示和输入闭包\n7     若 c>0，验证完整 READY 副本及取得源保护的合法路径\n8     若 c=0，验证初始化与重算输入，不要求虚构缓存命中\n9     展开新增状态、COW、接收、转换、增长和输出容量\n10    排除当前资源无法承诺的方案，不预支尚未排空的回收量\n11    构造依赖与资源时间线，估计到同一任务终点的 F_i\n12    排除违反硬截止时间或其他合同的方案\n13    将合法计划及快照版本加入 plans\n14 按预测完成时间排序；并列按预先固定的计划身份处理\n15 对排序后的计划，直到有限重试预算耗尽：\n16    重新核对请求、实例与计划版本；变化则重建相关候选\n17    取得所需源对象保护；失败则尝试下一个计划\n18    在目标资源协调域中原子复核容量与代次并预留新增资源\n19    预留失败：撤销本次尚无在途访问的临时保护，继续下一计划\n20    发布绑定 R、η、e、恢复点及目标 incarnation 的准入决定\n21    返回 ADMIT；实际执行仍等待全部输入、状态和执行依赖就绪\n22 无计划成功：若 now 小于固定等待终点且合同允许等待，返回有界 WAIT\n23 否则 REJECT，并保留容量、身份、输入、路径或截止时间原因\n```\n\n源保护由源所属域兑现，目标容量事务由目标资源域兑现；不能把跨实例的查表描述为天然原子。成功取得源保护后若目标预留失败，应结算临时责任。已经提交访问时，撤销计划先停止新访问，再进入 DRAINING，不能直接释放。\n\n多路由器同时看到同一空闲节点时，稳定并列规则只保证可复现，甚至可能让它们同时选中该节点。真正防止超额承诺的是预留时的资源复核；按请求稳定散列、局部竞争反馈或随机分散是降低竞争的策略。WAIT 的截止点必须跨重试保留，不能每次调用重新延长预算。\n\n设扫描 N 个实例，每个实例有至多 P 个计划，单计划身份与清单检查量为 H，直接枚举约需 O(NPH) 工作，排序为 O(NP log(NP))；候选与责任记录占 O(NP) 主机空间。缓存不可变身份可以减少重复检查，当前源存活、保护和容量资格仍须重新成立。\n\n## 活跃迁移的分阶段算法\n\n在线预复制要求被复制的旧状态不再改写。普通全注意力 decoder 已完成 KV 常满足逻辑 append-only；窗口覆盖、SSM 递归、未封闭量化组、投机回滚和物理尾页整理可能破坏它。应建立不可变快照或 COW，计入额外容量与转换；无法建立稳定快照时，改为暂停复制、合法重放或放弃在线迁移。\n\nLlumnix v1 §4.2 在每阶段前请求目标预分配，成功 ACK、失败 ABORT；阶段间检查源请求是否完成或被抢占，最后暂停请求并复制剩余状态。本章将这些组织展开为显式前沿与授权边界。以下基线目标初始为空，源位置 p0 的完整历史尚未复制；目标已有前缀时先验证并保护它，再将复制前沿初始化为该共同位置。\n\n预复制阶段数上限 max_stages 是非负整数，计入已经启动的阶段；零表示禁止启动任何预复制阶段。最终冻结、补齐差量与接管另有容量和停顿预算。下面选择明确的中止策略：差量仍超过 ε 而预复制预算不允许继续时，在冻结前中止本次迁移。暂停复制后备方案必须作为另一个显式计划重新核算，不能隐含地绕过这一预算分支。\n\n```pseudocode\n算法 MIGRATE_APPEND_ONLY(R,s,d,p0,plan)\n输入：固定身份、稳定快照能力、max_stages≥0、ε≥0、字节/时间预算\n输出：目标唯一接管；或中止、待恢复及待排空责任\n\n1 核对源 owner、η、端点 incarnation、表示、共同位置和输出记录\n2 建立 tx；u←0；m←0；T_pre←0；X_pre←0；源完整位置初值为 p0\n\n阶段入口，首次及每次继续追赶都执行：\n3 若源终止、抢占、失去授权或任一端失败，转预冻结中止\n4 在完整模型步骤边界取得源当前位置 v，固定稳定范围 [u,v)\n5 D←该范围实际状态字节；核对 u≤v 及目标 [0,u) 仍完整有效\n6 若 D≤ε，直接转最终冻结；不预留、不启动任何预复制阶段\n7 若 m≥max_stages，转预冻结中止\n8 核对复制 D 所需的下一阶段字节和时间额度；不足则转预冻结中止\n9 取得本阶段预算额度，再向目标申请接收、转换、最终状态和增长容量\n10 目标预留失败则结算未用额度并中止；成功后登记读写及暂存责任\n11 m←m+1；复制固定范围 [u,v)，源可以继续追加\n12 等待本阶段完整范围与必要可见性；失败不推进 u，转预冻结中止\n13 成功后 u←v；将实际阶段时间与复制字节加入 T_pre、X_pre\n14 返回阶段入口步骤 3，重新判断差量，不能直接启动下一次复制\n\n最终冻结与准备：\n15 在权威域验证 tx 与源授权，关闭源的新提交入口\n16 等待或结算源已在途步骤，在完整步骤边界固定最终 p、A、ticket 和控制状态\n17 权威记录 FROZEN，撤销源旧 e 的提交权；源保留恢复依据\n18 按冻结后的实际 [u,p) 核对最终容量、停顿及剩余预算；失败转冻结后异常\n19 为最终接收、转换和状态补齐取得容量；登记责任后复制最终差量与 manifest\n20 等待全部状态族及消费者依赖，核对恰好恢复 S(p)，建立 PREPARED\n21 在同一权威域复核请求未终止、冻结版本及目标仍有效，提交目标新授权\n22 目标确认权威决定后开始推进；源不再恢复旧授权\n23 按恢复合同移交保留责任，各物理对象在最后使用结束后回收\n\n预冻结中止：\n24 关闭本 tx 的新复制和发布；源若仍是合法 owner 则继续承担请求\n25 清理未使用预留和额度；已接受或接受未知访问进入排空，不发布部分目标\n26 返回 ABORT 或 DRAINING，保留已经发生的时间、字节与访问责任\n\n冻结后异常：\n27 查询权威决定；确认丢失不意味着未提交，不重复发放授权\n28 确定未接管时，以新的权威决定恢复源或另建恢复；不得自行恢复旧 e\n29 无完整恢复依据则明确失败；物理排空责任继续保留\n```\n\n入口顺序决定合法路径：先确认源责任，再判断差量，仍需追赶时才判断阶段预算。即使 max_stages=0，只要初始 D≤ε，仍可直接申请最终冻结与接管；这不代表免除最终容量或停顿检查。初始 D>ε 且 max_stages=0 时，本基线直接中止，没有预复制预留、数据传输或阶段固定开销。每次复制完成后都回到相同入口，因此最后允许阶段刚好使 D≤ε 时，应进入最终冻结，而不是因阶段计数已满误报中止。\n\n字节额度按固定范围的真实表示核算。时间额度若依赖预测，应说明其误差或保守上界；没有可兑现的上界就不能保证硬时限。阶段超时只关闭新工作并转异常处理，已经接受的访问仍可能需要额外排空时间。失败阶段消耗的实际资源也必须保留，不能因 u 未推进就从成本账中删除。\n\n初始化 u=0 很关键：首个被允许的预复制阶段须覆盖初始历史，不能把 u 初始化为 p0 后只复制新增长范围。若入口已达阈值而跳过预复制，初始历史仍在最终 [u,p) 中，不会因此漏传。阶段上界 v 在复制前固定；源后来追加的状态只进入下一入口的差量，不能在一次复制中无版本地追读不断变化的尾部。\n\n最终冻结发生在测量差量之后，因此 ε 只是决定何时尝试冻结的阈值。冻结后的实际差量、容量和停顿预算要再核对。按 token 或页离散增长时，必须使用完整步骤的真实边界；不能为了满足连续模型中的 ε 截断一个必需状态包。\n\n| 阶段 | 可以发生 | 不允许的替代 |\n|---|---|---|\n| 预复制 | 源追加；目标持有未发布状态 | 部分层到达即执行 |\n| FROZEN | 无旧授权新提交；保留完整恢复依据 | 源暂停后立即删除最后副本 |\n| PREPARED | 目标完整可见，等待权威决定 | READY 自动获得输出权 |\n| 接管后 | 目标新授权推进；旧操作按原责任结算 | 迟到源结果修改目标进度 |\n| 中止/取消 | 停止新工作，保留未决访问 | 业务终止即物理释放 |\n\n迁移性能机制不等于故障恢复保证。Llumnix §5 明确实例失效会中止该实例请求；组件重启不保证活跃请求无缝恢复。本章的授权、确认丢失和恢复责任是独立设计边界，不能归称为论文已有事务接口。\n\n## 追赶条件、停顿与全过程成本\n\n考虑连续增长、固定有效带宽的串行阶段模型。D_k 为阶段入口尚未复制的完整状态字节；B>0 为字节/秒，g≥0 为源新增字节/秒，此处 g 是增长速率而非执行尝试代次；α≥0 为允许启动后的阶段固定开销，包含准备、预留和完成等待期间源仍继续增长的时间。入口检查耗时在此简化为零；实际控制开销应另计或纳入相应增长区间。仅当入口允许预复制时，才发生\n\nt_k=α+D_k/B，D_{k+1}=g t_k=rD_k+a，r=g/B，a=gα。\n\n独立展开为\n\nD_m=r^mD_0+aΣ_{j=0}^{m−1}r^j。\n\nr≠1 时，第二项为 a(1−r^m)/(1−r)；r=1 时 D_m=D_0+ma。这里 D_0=0 仍有空状态的控制与接管责任，但无需虚构数据预复制。\n\n当 D_0>ε 且 B≤g 时，非负开销下差量不减，原预复制策略不能达到更小阈值。B>g 时，固定点为\n\nD∞=gα/(1−g/B)，D_m=D∞+r^m(D_0−D∞)。\n\n从 D_0>ε 向下追赶时，ε>D∞ 才能在有限阶段到达；ε=D∞ 通常只是渐近边界。α=0 且 0<r<1 时，可由 D_0r^m≤ε 求所需阶段数；阶段数还必须落在预算内。g=0 时一次预复制后没有新增差量；初始 D_0≤ε 时可直接走最终暂停阶段，不要求 B>g。\n\n执行 m 个预复制阶段后，若决定最终暂停并接管，令最终阶段固定开销为 α_f、接管为 h：\n\nT_pre=Σ_{k=0}^{m−1}(α+D_k/B)，\nT_down=α_f+D_m/B+h，T_wall=T_pre+T_down，\nX=Σ_{k=0}^{m−1}D_k+D_m。\n\nX 是该简化模型的逻辑复制字节；源在预复制期间保持连续增长，最终冻结无额外增长间隙时，还有守恒关系 X=D_0+gT_pre。重传、编码、重复目标副本和 staging 访存另计。α_f 即使无数据也可能包含必要控制开销。\n\n若预算耗尽后选择中止，最终差量没有被复制，不能仍给它增加一次最终传输并称为成功迁移。中止前已复制字节只有 ΣD_k，已花时间为 T_pre；关闭、排空和恢复可能另有成本。暂停复制是另一条明确候选，其停顿可能远大于 ε/B，应单独计算并检查合同。\n\n下表把入口分支与时间、字节逐步对应。全部是连续模型输入，单位为字节和秒，固定 g=1、B=2、ε=1，α=α_f=h=0；没有模拟失败与额外控制耗时。\n\n| D_0 | max_stages | 入口与后继 | 预复制时间 | 预复制字节 | 最终暂停时间 | 总已传字节 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 10 | 0 | 10>1，阶段预算不允许，直接 ABORT | 0 | 0 | 0，未冻结 | 0 |\n| 1 | 0 | 1≤1，跳过预复制，直接冻结补齐 | 0 | 0 | 0.5 | 1 |\n| 10 | 1 | 允许复制 10，耗时 5，新增差量 5；下一入口预算耗尽，ABORT | 5 | 10 | 0，未冻结 | 10 |\n| 2 | 1 | 允许复制 2，耗时 1，新增差量 1；下一入口先满足阈值，冻结补齐 | 1 | 2 | 0.5 | 3 |\n\n第一行若先复制再查预算，就会错误消耗 5 秒、传输 10 字节。第二行若先查阶段预算而不查差量，就会错误拒绝合法的直接冻结路径；若无条件先复制，则多花 0.5 秒预复制并产生 0.5 字节新增状态。空和式规定 T_pre=X_pre=0，因此零阶段路径不支付 α；直接冻结仍支付真实存在的 α_f 与 h。\n\n设定 D_0=1000 MiB、g=10 MiB/s、B=100 MiB/s、α=0、ε=1 MiB。前三阶段复制 1000、100、10 MiB，耗时 10、1、0.1 s，剩余 1 MiB；最终数据暂停为 0.01 s，总时间为 11.11 s，总逻辑复制量为 1111 MiB，另加 α_f 与 h。这些是演算输入，不是设备测量。\n\n若 B 降到 8 MiB/s，五阶段后差量为 3051.7578125 MiB，预复制已花 1025.87890625 s、已复制 8207.03125 MiB。此时按中止策略没有执行最终暂停；若改为暂停复制，单最终数据搬运还需约 381.47 s。两个决策不能混成同一条成功记录。\n\n若 α=0.05 s、g=10 MiB/s、B=100 MiB/s，则 D∞=5/9 MiB≈0.556 MiB。ε=0.1 MiB 无法达到，即使 B>g；改变 α 或阈值的实验能区分固定开销与带宽不足。\n\n实际 B 与 g 会随其他迁移、远端缓存读取、模型通信、batch 和输出轨迹变化。离散页舍入与状态转换也会改变差量。非平稳阶段可写为 D_{k+1}=g_k(α_k+D_k/B_k)，但一个窗口中的 B_k>g_k 不能证明整个未来轨迹收敛。\n\n| 成本 | 同一候选中需要保留的工作 |\n|---|---|\n| 计算 | 身份与清单核验、候选扫描、未命中 prefill、后缀 Attention、转换、重放和失败工作 |\n| 访存 | 源状态读、打包与 staging 读写、目标写、页表、scale、转换区；逻辑字节不等于 HBM 事务 |\n| 通信 | 状态净荷、各目标副本、控制/ACK/ABORT、重传、输出及共享路径竞争 |\n| 准备 | 目录查询、源保护、容量预留、注册、描述构造、冷加载与执行组建立 |\n| 同步 | 阶段快照、预留确认、完整范围可见、最终冻结、唯一授权和输出提交 |\n| 驻留 | 源/目标重叠副本、未发布接收区、增长余量、暂存、元数据、未决操作和待排空旧对象 |\n\n源状态和目标状态会同时存在；预复制减少停顿，不减少这段重叠驻留。复制主项为 O(X) 字节工作，阶段控制为 O(m)，实际清单处理还随块、层、分片和目标数增长。物理峰值按同时存活分配并集计算，不能用 X 当作容量峰值。\n\n## 条件变化后的选择\n\n身份、共同恢复点、容量承诺、唯一提交权和最后使用条件保持不变；队列预测、亲和程度、阶段大小、复制阈值与热点副本数量可以调整。\n\n| 条件变化 | 重新比较或放弃的内容 |\n|---|---|\n| 前缀变短、只匹配不完整块 | 使用更短合法恢复点；比较冷重算与远端恢复 |\n| 命中实例排队增加 | 节省的计算可能小于新增排队，允许无命中节点胜出 |\n| B 下降、g 增大或 α 支配小阶段 | 重算固定点与预算；比较暂停复制、其他请求迁移、重放或中止 |\n| 阶段预算降为零 | 仍先检查当前差量；已达阈值可申请直接冻结，否则中止在线追赶 |\n| 模型含覆盖、递归或投机状态 | 重建共同快照；不能沿用普通 append-only 轨迹 |\n| 目标需要重分片或新表示 | 展开逻辑状态到目标分配的映射、转换与容量；详见状态迁移章 |\n| 热点副本共享同一出口 | 副本数增加未必增加总服务能力，重新计算链路与目标瓶颈 |\n| 高优先级隔离或实例排空 | 记录被迁请求的成本与各租户 SLO，不能只报告受益请求 |\n| 源/目标失效或确认丢失 | 按权威决定与恢复依据处理，不以超时推断未接管 |\n\n热点复制可用观察窗口内的预计复用次数 ν 比较：若每次减少的净计算与排队为 V、增加的取得成本为 F、后台复制和驻留挤出代价为 C，则在同一计价单位下比较 ν(V−F) 与 C。C 可以由资源向量或显式价格换成时间等价量，不能直接把 GiB 与 ms 相减。共享祖先的收益用边际价值，避免给多个对象重复记整段前缀收益。\n\n搬走一个大请求可能避免源端抢占，也可能比重新路由未来请求更昂贵；搬走多个小请求可能增加固定控制和接管成本。应比较同一观察窗口内预计避免的等待、干扰与重算，以及迁移给邻居和目标增加的成本，并保留预测误差余量，避免反复来回迁移。\n\n## 竞争解释与区分实验\n\n先固定任务终点、offered 请求人口和观察窗口，再关联逻辑匹配、实际跳过计算、源 READY、容量等待、传输、冻结和输出提交。高命中率或低停顿都不能单独证明端到端收益。\n\n| 观察 | 竞争解释 | 区分改变与可反驳预测 |\n|---|---|---|\n| 所有路由基例都 REJECT | 候选身份不匹配；容量全不足；截止时间过紧 | 固定成本，逐项展示合法集合与排除原因；只统一正确 η 后应恢复预期排名，不能删除 η 检查 |\n| 命中高但首内容慢 | 命中未成为共同状态；远端排队/传输；后缀计算支配 | 固定恢复位置，分别改变源队列、带宽和后缀长度；同时记录实际避免的计算 |\n| 零阶段预算仍有迁移净荷 | 入口预算检查过晚；旧尝试流量混入；最终暂停流量被误记 | 固定 tx，令 D_0>ε，检查首个预复制预留与复制\n\n[EXCERPT extracted-character range title/tags/questions and algorithm/mechanism keywords; ties retain source order."}, {"path": "knowledge/algorithms/kv-offload-prefetch.md", "sha256": "4b8d8bad42a337904a2630f9ecf2fb39b5b8d6142bb00aa51f43afb36a842b20", "reading_scope": "full", "category": "related_chapter", "content": "# K-ALG-018：KV 卸载与预取：读集合、恢复点与迁移预算\n\nKV 卸载需要同时回答存储在哪里、下一次 attention 会读哪些状态，以及何时能够恢复推进。暂停请求换出后恢复、低层前缀缓存命中和活跃 decode 的逐步卸载有不同的成本：前两者主要支付一次恢复费用，后者可能每步都搬运历史。释放显存、避免重复 prefill 和降低稳态流量必须分别判断。\n\n## 对象、读集合与恢复位置\n\n固定计算身份 η，包括模型与权重、adapter、实际输入前缀、位置规则、外部条件、数值表示和布局合同。令 p 为已完成模型消费的 token 数，S(p) 为消费 x[0:p) 后继续执行所需的状态包。普通全注意力 decoder 包含逐层历史 K/V；窗口、MLA、递归层和交叉注意力需要各自的状态。不同族必须支持同一个恢复位置，不能只比较长度字段。\n\nS(p) 通常不包含末行 logits、已采样未消费的 token、随机状态和输出游标。若已保留下一输入 x_p，可直接从 S(p) 消费它；完整命中 KV 而没有下一输入或末行 logits 时，不能自动采样。可以保存必要 logits，或从合法的较早恢复点重算末尾。递归状态不能通过把 p 减一恢复到旧检查点。生成任务的空状态也不自动提供合法起始输入。\n\n对层 l、位置 t 的 query，模型规定的可见位置为 A_{l,t}。当前位置 K/V 由当前层输入投影产生；历史读取集合为 H_{l,t}=A_{l,t}∩[0,t)。窗口宽 W≥1 在本章包含当前 token，故普通窗口的历史为 [max(0,t−W+1),t)；全注意力历史为 [0,t)。sink/global 等规则需要另外并入并去重，不能沿用纯窗口公式。\n\n规划从位置 p 开始的 q≥0 次连续 query 时，必须区分两个集合：\n\n- U(p,q)=∪_{t=p}^{p+q−1} H_t：这些步骤会读取过的全部历史位置，包含在本次推进中才生成、随后被读取的位置。\n- E(p,q)=U(p,q)∩[0,p)：规划开始时已经存在、可以从旧状态副本预取的位置。U\\E 必须由本次模型计算产生，不能从旧缓存复制。\n\nq=0 时二者为空。q>0 时，全注意力 U=[0,p+q−1)；普通窗口 W>1 时 U=[max(0,p−W+1),p+q−1)；W=1 时每个 query 只读自己，历史 U 恒为空。区间均取整数位置，空区间不产生读取。E 再与 [0,p) 相交。因此 q>0 的纯全注意力 E=[0,p)，纯窗口 E=[max(0,p−W+1),p)。未来读集合并集不是每步流量之和，同一旧位置可以在多个步骤重复读取。\n\n例如 p=10、q=1、W=4 时，U=E={7,8,9}，单次 query 不意味着空历史。p=0、q=3 的全注意力 U={0,1}，但 E 为空：前两步产生的状态供后续步使用。p=3、q=2、W=1 的 U 和 E 都为空。已知未来位置不等于已知未来 token；对自回归生成，后续输入仍要等实际采样或已有输入记录。\n\n输入包括 η、p、已知输入及继续票据、各层状态描述、页表、源与目的容量、传输能力和完成责任。输出为同一恢复位置的 RecoveryView，或明确失败。视图给出每族的物理句柄、代次、有效范围、表示、定位方式和就绪关系。下面分别讨论完整安装视图，以及以低层有效副本为后备、按层取得 GPU 工作视图的方案。\n\n## 布局、身份和可回收性\n\n页长 P 个 token 时，位置 j 的逻辑页为 floor(j/P)，页内槽为 j mod P。页表还要绑定层、K/V、head 分片、物理池和分配代次；两个池中的同号页不是同一对象。共享引用只有在内容、表示和生存期相容时才可映射到同一目的副本。相同源需要写到两个独立目的分配时，仍需支付两次目的写入，不能按源身份去重掉一个需求。\n\n一层等宽 K/V、H_kv 个 head、每 head 宽 d、每元素 s 字节时，每 token 有效载荷 τ=2H_kvds。紧密 NHD 页内，K 元素 (j,h,c) 的地址为 K_base+physical_page·D_K+s·(((j mod P)·H_kv+h)·d+c)，D_K 为按字节计的页 stride；V 使用自己的基址和 stride。布局改变时必须搬移或转换字节，不能只改描述符。量化还须定位码、scale、zero point、分组轴和必要残差。\n\n取 P=4、A 页表 [7,1,3]、p=10。位置 0…3 在页 7，4…7 在页 1，8、9 在页 3 的前两个槽。下一 query 使用 W=4 时，旧历史为 {7,8,9}，对应页 {1,3}，页 7 不需要换入。若 H_kv=2、d=4、s=2，则 τ=32 B：精确有效范围为 96 B，按整页搬两页则为 256 B。后者包含过读，空槽即使被搬运也不能进入 attention。\n\n另一请求 B 的页表为 [7,1,9]，也完成到 10。完整恢复两请求时，逻辑页引用为 3+3=6，唯一物理页为 {7,1,3,9}，即 4 页。整页目标占用为 512 B，独立六页为 768 B；唯一有效内容为共享 8 个位置加两个各长 2 的私有尾部，共 384 B。逐 token 展开有 20 个逻辑引用，它既不是六个逻辑页引用，也不是唯一有效 token 数。\n\n共享尾块的写入必须隔离。例如 A、B 完成到 6，共享页 [7,1]，页 1 只有前两个槽有效。B 要生成位置 6 时，先建立私有页 9、复制页 1 的两个有效槽及解释元数据，完成后再写 B 的新槽；A 仍使用页 1。若 A 已有设备读者，B 撤销自己的共享引用不能释放 A 的页。引用计数为一也不自动允许覆盖仍被旧快照读者保护的范围。\n\n把状态分成容量预留、传输在途、内容完整、获取开放和读者责任更清楚。RESERVED 不代表数据到达；COPYING 不代表完成；READY 表示本代完整内容对约定消费者可见。多个消费者可同时持有 READY 内容，读取中不应阻止其他合法只读获取。关闭获取后禁止新读者，已有读者继续受保护；只有拥有者、已提交复制和全部读者责任都结束，才可回收。\n\n身份检查与读者登记必须同回收协调，避免检查成功后、登记之前对象已被换代。代次检查用于拒绝迟到通知和错误结算；它不能阻止已经发出的旧 DMA 写入地址。保护物理内容仍依赖旧访问真实结束，或使用另一块隔离存储。\n\n## 换出、完整恢复与有界预取\n\n换出基线在完整模型步骤边界固定 S(p)，复制期间源不可变。普通追加历史可能稳定，但正在封组的量化尾部会重编码旧值或更新 scale；这种尾部必须冻结或制作独立快照。复制源的读保护从提交前保持到真实复制读取结束；最后一份恢复副本的保留责任还可能更长。\n\n```pseudocode\n输入：固定 η、恢复位置 p、完整状态清单 M、旧有效视图 V\n      目标层容量、布局合同和请求继续票据\n输出：目标 RecoveryView，或失败及待排空责任\n\n校验 M 覆盖全部必需状态族、有效范围和共同 p\n固定源快照，取得源保护；建立私有目标描述和空完成账\n预留完整安装所需的目标分配、元数据和转换空间\n若任一预留失败：撤销未使用预留，保留 V，返回失败\n\n对每个唯一目标物理需求 w：\n    将逻辑范围映射到源、目的字节区间及辅助元数据\n    若复用已有目标副本：原子核验身份并登记持有\n    否则先登记操作责任，再提交复制或转换\n    同步拒绝仅撤销明确未接受的责任\n    部分接受或接受状态未知时，保持对应源、目的和暂存保护\n\n处理完成：\n    按尝试、操作、分配代次匹配；未知或重复通知不贡献覆盖\n    记录实际成功范围；短范围不能补成完整范围\n    等待全部必需复制、转换及消费者可见性成立\n    任一失败或取消：禁止发布，停止新工作，排空已提交访问\n                    只归还已经无访问责任的私有分配，保留旧视图\n\n提交：\n    在协调点重新核验 η、p、全部范围、表示、持有权和请求资格\n    同时安装完整目标映射与继续票据，发布一次目标视图\n    撤销本次不再需要的源持有；仍受其他读者或恢复责任保护的源不释放\n```\n\nD2H 换出遵循同一关系：目标主存或存储副本完成、验证并承担约定恢复责任后，才能移除源恢复责任。主机提交返回不等于 DMA 结束；写入页缓存也不等于满足持久性。可丢弃后重算的缓存与必须保存的检查点应声明不同合同。H2D 完整恢复则先安装全部必需层，再允许下一次 forward；恢复过程不执行尚未发布的层计算。\n\n完整安装需要容纳完整目标状态。若目标只能容纳少数层，采用另一方案：低层后备状态先具备完整身份与恢复范围，GPU 只有 k 个工作槽。请求绑定的是后备对象和取用计划，逐层临时句柄仅在相应复制完成后交给本层消费者；全局视图不能保存指向已经循环复用槽的静态页表。\n\n下面限定普通因果 decoder 的单 token 推进。已提交历史不可变，新增 KV 使用私有候选；全部层完成才发布 S(p+1)。窗口、递归或会改写历史的量化状态，须先把相应候选更新和回滚责任补齐，不能直接套用 append-only 基线。\n\n```pseudocode\n输入：已绑定的后备 S(p)、合法下一输入 x_p、k≥1 个工作槽\n输出：完整步骤结果与 S(p+1)，或保留 S(p) 的失败\n\n初始化本步私有新增状态、层输出和操作账；不改变已提交 p\n生产游标 i←0；消费游标 l←0\n\n重复直到所有层消费完成，或发生失败：\n    生产者：按下一消费层顺序检查可用槽\n        槽须无旧复制、旧读者及尚未完成的写回\n        核验本层历史 E(p,1)，建立本代定位描述和源保护\n        先登记责任再发起换入及必要转换，推进 i\n        槽不足时先处理完成或等待最后使用，不等待整个模型全部换入\n    完成处理：匹配本代操作，验证范围和表示，标记本层工作视图 READY\n    消费者：当第 l 层历史就绪且上层隐藏输入就绪时\n        原子核验并取得全部读取票据\n        由本层隐藏输入生成 Q 与当前位置 K/V\n        对历史及当前位置按模型可见集合共同归一化，完成本层计算\n        真实读取结束后释放历史读者，保留本层输出给下一层\n        将新增 K/V 安装到本步私有持久位置，或完成必要写回\n        写回仍读取工作槽时，该槽继续受保护\n        最后使用结束后归还槽，推进 l\n\n成功：全部层新增状态、最终输出和必要写回就绪\n      复核请求资格，共同发布 p+1 及完整结果\n失败/取消：停止新提交，丢弃未发布业务结果；排空已提交访问\n          释放私有候选，旧 S(p) 及其他请求的共享状态保持有效\n```\n\n源端持有全部层的后备副本，不要求全部层同时驻留 GPU。仅通过 READY 的本层输入可在私有步骤中被消费；这不等于把部分恢复状态发布给其他 forward。若允许多步同时在途，还需新的依赖、候选版本及容量模型，本基线不作该假设。\n\n活跃卸载也必须保存新 KV。只搬回历史、计算后直接覆盖工作槽，会丢失下一步需要的新增状态。可把新 KV 直接写到独立 GPU 常驻区域，或写回主机后再释放槽；这两种选择分别增加设备驻留或写回交通。失败时若原地覆盖了旧量化组或递归状态，保留旧 p 字段不能实现回滚。\n\n## 六类成本与流水约束\n\n对等宽、非共享、无分片复制的一批普通全注意力状态，D=2LB_rNH_kvds 字节。变长请求改为逐请求、逐层求和；窗口层用实际保留或读取范围，额外 sink/global 单独并入。物理副本、页尾、padding 和量化元数据另计。这个 D 不是 GPU 的 DRAM 事务量。\n\n| 成本 | 同一计划内需要计入的对象 |\n|---|---|\n| 计算 | attention 与逐位置网络通常不减少；增加解包、反量化、转置、校验及重算；CPU attention 另计点积、整体归约和结果合并 |\n| 访存 | 源读、目的写、staging、页表、转换副本、新 KV 和写回；共享目标一份不保证各 query 的读取自动合并 |\n| 通信 | 实际经过存储、DRAM、H2D、D2H 或网络的载荷、元数据及目的副本；同一资源上所有需求相加 |\n| 准备 | 读集合规划、身份核验、分页映射、目标需求去重、分配、锁页或注册、描述构造、完成处理及回滚 |\n| 同步 | 消费前数据就绪、层间隐藏状态、完整步骤提交、最后读取和失败排空；并发等待不能重复相加 |\n| 驻留 | 有效后备、工作槽、私有新状态、旧快照、staging、转换空间、页尾、描述和未排空对象 |\n\n规划遍历 J 个逻辑页引用、形成 K 个唯一目标需求时，使用散列表去重在通常假设下约 O(J)，不能把它写成只与 K 有关：重复引用也要被检查。本文集合公式可用区间表示；逐 token 枚举和 q 步逐项求并集是检查参考，不是高效实现的强制成本。\n\n对存储层 h，容量应写为 M_h,peak=max_t Σ_{a∈Live_h(t)} bytes(a)，其中 a 是唯一物理分配。一个目的页从 RESERVED 到 COPYING 仍是同一分配，不能把两种角色各加一次；池 reserved 已包含其 active 槽时也不重复相加。不同存储层分别对照容量；将主存、设备和 SSD 总字节合成一个预算没有准入意义。\n\n暂停恢复的简单串行路径为排队、读取、传输、转换和发布等待之和；允许流水时按真实依赖求关键路径。对共享资源 r，每次工作实际产生 Q_r 字节，能力上界 β_r 给出 T≥Q_r/β_r 的资源下界；代入测得的有效速率则只是对应并发条件的估计。两条 stream 不会创造两份 PCIe 或 DRAM 带宽。\n\n考虑一个只读层输入模型：每层换入用一个串行复制引擎，计算用一个串行引擎，k 个循环工作槽从换入开始占用到计算最后读取结束。设 m_i、c_i 为非负持续时间，C_i 为换入完成，F_i 为计算完成，负下标时间取零：\n\nC_i=max(C_{i−1},F_{i−k})+m_i，\n\nF_i=max(C_i,F_{i−1})+c_i。\n\n第一项表达复制引擎顺序，第二项表达槽的最后使用；消费者同时等本层数据与上一层计算。n=0 时完成时间为零，不等待最后票据。n>0 时总时间 F_{n−1}，各层暴露等待为 max(0,C_i−F_{i−1})。这个模型要求层输入可独立准备、源稳定且两种引擎能按假设服务。\n\n四层 c=[5,5,5,5]、m=[3,3,3,3]、k=2，换入完成为 [3,6,11,16]，计算完成为 [8,13,18,23]，除首层外没有暴露等待；k=1 则总时间 32。把 m 改成 [8,8,8,8]，k=2 的总时间为 37，不能仍用 sum(c)+m_0=28；28 甚至小于同一复制引擎必须服务的 32。增加槽数不能消除持续带宽不足。\n\n如果工作槽还保存待写回的新状态，释放点应改为真实写回完成 R_i，换入式变为 C_i=max(C_{i−1},R_{i−k})+m_i。独立串行写回资源下可再写 R_i=max(F_i,R_{i−1})+w_i；若 H2D/D2H 共用瓶颈，则必须使用共享资源顺序与联合带宽，不能假定两个独占引擎。这些附加责任没有包含在前述 23/37 的只读模型中。\n\n设定 512 MiB 暂停状态、16 GiB/s H2D，纯数据项为 31.25 ms；同一恢复边界的重算若为 20 ms，命中不意味着更快；重算若为 200 ms，恢复才有潜在优势，仍需加排队、转换与同步。2 GiB/s SSD 读取同样状态的数据项为 250 ms。这些时间均为设定值。\n\n再取 L=32、B_r=8、N=4096、H_kv=8、d=128、s=2，完整历史为 4 GiB，每层 128 MiB。16 GiB/s 下每层纯换入为 7.8125 ms，全部数据为 250 ms。若每层计算设为 20 ms，满足上述只读双槽条件时总时间为 647.8125 ms；它包含首层换入，且大于 250 ms 的链路资源下界。完整 KV 可常驻时，理想计算对照为 640 ms。真实活跃卸载还须计新增写回、权重流量、准备和拥塞，不能把这个只读流水当作完整 decode 实测。\n\nCPU attention 可以保留主存历史、交换 query/激活和结果，以避免整段 KV H2D，但 CPU 点积、softmax、NUMA、主存争用和同步进入关键路径。分页组织交通，量化改变字节及解码成本，CPU 委托改变计算放置；三者不能互相替代，也没有统一最优组合。\n\n## 条件变化、反例和选择反转\n\n| 条件变化 | 必须保留的关系 | 调整或放弃的依据 |\n|---|---|---|\n| 前缀长且重复计算昂贵 | 同一身份、共同恢复点和继续票据 | 比较完整恢复等待与重算；短前缀或冷存储可能反转 |\n| 活跃历史持续增长 | 模型实际可见集合 | 全注意力逐步流量增长；窗口层不能代替全层预算 |\n| W 变为 1 或 p=0 | 当前 KV 的生产仍存在 | 旧历史可为空；不再发起空历史传输，但仍完成当前层计算 |\n| 共享前缀增多 | 目的需求去重、独立读者与 COW | 唯一容量可能下降；kernel 的逻辑读取不保证同比下降 |\n| 页长增大 | 有效范围、尾槽和布局合同 | 表项和小传输可能减少，过读、碎片及 COW 粒度上升 |\n| 量化或布局改变 | 码与解释元数据一致 | 重算转换、临时副本和误差；旧字节相同不证明新表示合法 |\n| 链路拥塞或层计算缩短 | 复制完成先于消费 | 重新求流水；持续带宽不足时减少卸载、改放置或限准入 |\n| 目标容量减少 | 在途及旧读者保护 | 缩小预取深度或等待；不能通过提前归还槽制造容量 |\n| 取消、部分失败或完成延迟 | 禁止新业务提交与排空分别处理 | 保留旧视图，等待真实最后访问；换代次不代替排空 |\n| 模型、位置规则或 adapter 改变 | 状态依赖闭包 | 明确转换并验证，或放弃旧状态复用 |\n\n非整页前缀不是普遍非法。页长 16、完成到 31 时，允许尾部迁移的协议可以复制最后 15 个有效槽和元数据，恢复到 31；只允许完整缓存页的协议最多复用到 16，剩余 15 个位置重算。把完整页粒度方案的 16 当成所有卸载的恢复上限，会无故丢失复用；把未复制尾部的描述写成 31，则会读到 poison 或旧请求数据。\n\n共享分叉也须观察真实内容：只核对新页号不同，不能证明保留槽已复制；只核对 B 的新增槽，不能证明 A 的旧页未被改。应同时检查 A 的历史、B 的保留历史、新槽及各自有效长度。\n\n发布与回收的反例具有不同后果。缺一层就发布，得到不完整模型输入；COPYING 时开放获取，读到未完成内容；取消后提前归还目的槽，再让旧 DMA 写入，会污染新对象。最后一种错误即使完成通知被代次检查拒绝，物理污染仍已发生。故负对照必须实际复用存储并让旧写到达，不能只检查一个错误状态标签。\n\n## 区分实验与检查范围\n\n| 观察 | 竞争解释 | 区分实验及反证后的修正 |\n|---|---|---|\n| 恢复慢 | 冷存储、链路、转换、准备或同步 | 固定有效范围与并发，比较热内存、DRAM、SSD；热路径仍慢时撤回纯 SSD 解释，分查转换与消费者等待 |\n| 深预取无收益 | 槽不足、资源饱和、首层等待或计算主导 | 固定工作量改变 k，记录槽占用、复制起止和消费时间；峰值增加而等待不降时减少深度 |\n| 命中却不能生成 | 共同状态不齐、缺下一输入或 logits | 分记匹配位置、可恢复位置和继续票据；补齐合法末行路径后再比较 TTFT |\n| 共享后容量下降但 ITL 不变 | 唯一页减少，各 query 仍读取；或计算、排队主导 | 固定准入请求和输入比较实际传输与 kernel 读取，再单独开放容量准入；不能用容量比例推断带宽收益 |\n| 跨页或尾部错误 | 页映射、有效长度、COW 复制或元数据遗漏 | 置换物理页并用 poison 填空槽，独立逻辑参考逐位置核对；定位首次错误槽和表示参数 |\n| 取消或复用后串数据 | 旧 DMA、未结束读者、旧描述或重复完成 | 分别延迟复制与读者，注入旧代/重复通知；记录实际内容与责任，区别账务错误和物理覆盖 |\n| 量化后省字节却更慢 | 解包、重编码、工作区或 CPU 委托退化 | 固定输入和质量合同，分别记录码/参数交通、转换与计算；转换主导时修订低位必快的判断 |\n\n活跃 decode 对照应固定历史、batch、模型和完成边界，比较全驻留、逐步卸载、CPU attention 与合法分片放置，报告每步实际字节、暴露等待、ITL、预取浪费和分层峰值。全设备同步使错误消失，只说明某些执行关系改变，不能唯一定位缺失依赖，也不能作为正常性能对照。\n\n配套 tools/check_k_alg_018_full.py 是确定、离线、只读的 Python 标准库 CPU 模型。读集合用逐 query、逐位置谓词枚举对照区间公式，同时检查 U 和 E；页账分别使用逻辑页编号、唯一物理页和逐槽内容集合；共享尾页执行实际复制和写入，并保留不做 COW 的内容污染负对照。\n\n生命周期检查由独立事件观察器重建未完成复制、拥有者、获取资格和多个读者；覆盖取消、未知/重复完成、旧代通知及安全复用。提前开放、带读者回收、带在途复制回收的错误轨迹必须被检出；旧写污染另在内存模型中实际发生。完整安装检查覆盖全部层、身份、范围及辅助元数据的共同提交。流水递推与按整数时刻推进的独立引擎/槽模型比较，包含空任务、零持续时间、单槽、多槽和首尾排空。\n\n这些检查覆盖普通全注意力、无额外 sink/global 的纯窗口集合、单层分页示例、有限生命周期和固定时间流水。它们不执行真实模型、文件 I/O、GPU、DMA、PCIe、锁页、设备可见性或多机授权；不证明低精度误差、真实事务合并、任意混合状态回滚、实际重叠和端到端性能。三阶段写回关系在正文中给出，配套流水穷举仅验证两阶段只读模型。检查执行是否通过由绑定当前脚本和输入的真实执行记录承担。\n\n<!-- Caching](../sources/S-HF-CACHE.md)：固定截止版本文档说明逐层复用历史 K/V、计算并合并新增 K/V，以及 mask 覆盖历史和新增 token；并区分 Dynamic、Static、StaticSlidingWindow 等形态。本文据此区分旧状态与当前生产路径，不把文档字段当作通用 ABI。文中乘 mask 示意不能替代不可见项从 softmax 实测。来源版本上限为 2026-04-30；编辑日期不改变来源时间。\n\n## 延伸阅读\n\n- [多级驻留：有效副本、预取、淘汰与重算](../algorithms/tiered-residency.md)。\n- [分页 KV：逻辑位置、物理布局与共享所有权](../algorithms/paged-kv-capacity.md)。\n- [前缀缓存：计算等价类、Radix 与淘汰收益](../algorithms/prefix-cache-identity.md)。\n- [异构状态缓存：层组、恢复位置与联合分配](../algorithms/heterogeneous-state-cache.md)。\n- [分布式状态迁移：身份、传输与提交点](../system-semantics/distributed-state-transfer.md)。\n- [CPU–GPU 计算放置：传数据、传结果与共同提交](../algorithms/heterogeneous-execution.md)。\n- [内存分配：地址、容量与在途所有权](../system-semantics/memory-allocators.md)。\n", "excerpt_ranges": [[0, 11529]], "range_unit": "zero-based half-open characters in extracted text", "extracted_characters": 11529, "selected_characters": 11529, "extraction": "UTF-8 text", "selection_reason": "full extracted text fits allocation"}, {"path": "knowledge/algorithms/kv-compression.md", "sha256": "6a90b569f9fd1b74ca2e8ede11f1094bcb15df5ff6918d4c2d357b9c5dbc1e68", "reading_scope": "excerpt", "category": "related_chapter", "content": "[EXCERPT extracted-character range 0:8654]\n# K-ALG-021：KV 压缩：表示、近似保留与传输格式\n\nKV 量化、丢弃历史和压缩传输减少的是不同成本。设计应说明模型实际消费什么状态、哪些信息改变，以及节省的字节发生在驻留、读取还是网络边界。压缩比不能独立决定方案；还要计入表示转换、统计、临时副本和后续 attention 的成本。\n\n## 问题、函数与语义边界\n\n先考虑一层、一个 KV head，K、V∈ℝ^{L×d}，L 是已完成的逻辑 token 数，d 是通道数。本章为便于展开取 K/V 同宽；不同宽度时，分别使用 d_K、d_V。对 query q_t 和模型规定的可见集合 A_t，定义\n\nz_j=a·q_t·K_j＋β_{t,j}，p_j=exp(z_j−m)/Σ_{i∈A_t}exp(z_i−m)，o_t=Σ_{j∈A_t}p_jV_j，\n\n其中 a 是模型缩放，β 是位置偏置，m=max_{j∈A_t}z_j。不可见项不进入分母。本章有限输入模型规定空集合输出零并标记为空，不计算空最大值或除零；实际接口须明确自己的空行合同。\n\n| 机制 | 改变的对象 | 需要验证的关系 |\n|---|---|---|\n| KV 低位量化 | K/V 的数值表示 | 固定制品的执行正确性、近似误差和任务质量 |\n| 重要 token 保留 | 本次实际参与 attention 的历史子集 | 新的近似函数、未来依赖和长期质量 |\n| 压缩传输/存储 | 序列化字节及恢复路径 | 熵解码、量化重建、身份与端到端成本 |\n| 模型原生窗口 | 模型本来规定的 A_t | 既定窗口、位置与保留语义是否被保持 |\n\n淘汰暂不使用的跨请求缓存副本，通常允许以后重算；直接删除当前请求本应可见的历史，则改变模型函数。量化保持位置集合但改变重建值。熵编码可以相对整数码无损，前面的量化仍然有损，不能据此称整条管线无损。\n\n输入除了数值，还包含模型/权重与前缀身份、层和 head、位置规则、格式版本、分组轴、码域、舍入、有效范围及物理映射。输出是可供消费者读取的码、参数、残差或保留集合，以及共同完成的状态视图。已分配、已写入、已封组、已发布和仍被读者持有是不同事实，不能用一个长度字段代替。\n\n## 分组量化的数值合同\n\n对一组有限数 x_i，码域为整数 [q_min,q_max]，q_min<q_max。本章选择最近舍入 R(u)=floor(u＋1/2)，半整数向正无穷取整；这是明确的检查合同，不声称所有 KIVI 实现采用同一 tie 规则。实数偏移格式为\n\nq_i=clip(R((x_i−b)/s),q_min,q_max)，x̂_i=b＋s q_i。\n\n若用 min/max 覆盖整组范围，非恒定组必须取\n\ns=(max x−min x)/(q_max−q_min)，b=min x−s q_min。\n\n只有 q_min=0 时才能简写 b=min x。把这个简写直接用于有符号码域会错配重建区间并裁剪高端。例如 x=[−1,0,2]、码域 [−2,1] 时，正确 s=1、b=1，可以精确重建三个值；错误 b=−1 会把 2 裁成重建值 0。执行与参考若同时复制错误公式，往返自洽仍可能掩盖缺陷。\n\n常数组 c 选择合法码 q_c=q_min、s=1、b=c−q_c，精确重建；空组拒绝。另一种格式 s(q−z) 使用整数零点 z，不能把实数偏移 b 当作 z。NaN、Inf、保存后为零或非有限的尺度应拒绝或进入明确的高精度例外。实际制品须先冻结保存精度下的参数，再编码；数学参数经过保存舍入后不能继续配旧码而声称同一重建值。\n\n```pseudocode\n输入：非空有限向量 x，整数码域 [qmin,qmax]，明确的参数保存格式\n输出：整数码 q、正尺度 s、实数偏移 b\n\n若 qmin≥qmax 或输入不合法：拒绝\n若 min(x)=max(x)：\n    qc←qmin；s←1；b←min(x)−qc\n否则：\n    s←(max(x)−min(x))/(qmax−qmin)\n    b←min(x)−s·qmin\n将 s、b 转成实际保存格式并冻结\n若冻结后的 s 非有限或不为正，或 b 非有限：拒绝\n对每个元素 x_i：\n    q_i←clip(floor((x_i−b)/s＋1/2),qmin,qmax)\n消费者按冻结的 s、b 重建 b＋s·q_i\n```\n\n在精确参数、无裁剪且最近舍入的模型下，单元素误差不超过 s/2；实际浮点运算和参数保存另有误差。attention 的误差还受 query 方向影响：Δz_j=a·q_t·ΔK_j，故 |Δz_j|≤|a|·||q_t||₂·||ΔK_j||₂。同一可见集合内，若 ||V_j||≤M，则\n\n||ô−o||≤M·||p̂−p||₁＋max_j||ΔV_j||。\n\nK 的重建误差先改变 softmax，V 的误差再进入加权和。较小的 KV 均方误差不直接保证较小的输出误差，更不能保证自回归轨迹或任务答案不变。\n\n## KIVI 式流式表示：两个轴和两个残差边界\n\n[KIVI v1](../sources/S-KIVI.md) §3 与附录 A 的机制是：K 固定 channel，跨 token 分组；V 固定 token，沿 channel 分组。对 K，参数身份为 (层、KV head、token 组 g、channel c)，g=floor(j/G)；对 V，身份为 (层、KV head、token j、channel 组 h)，h=floor(c/G)。名称 per-channel 描述参数属于哪个通道，不能理解为把不同通道任意混成同一组。\n\nK 的离群通道观察解释了为什么隔离通道可能减少其他通道的网格损失；V 的 per-token 分组则使其他 token 的范围不直接改变某个重要 token 的量化网格。这是论文评估模型上的经验依据，不是所有架构、head 或 latent 表示的最佳轴定理。\n\n选择正整数 G、R，且 R 是 G 的倍数。K 的残差是凑组缓冲：追加后达到 R 个 token 就按 G 封组并清空。V 的残差是最近 R 个 token 的高精度队列：超过 R 时量化最旧行。对从空状态顺序追加并完成封组的长度 L，有\n\nr_K=L mod R，n_K=L−r_K；r_V=min(L,R)，n_V=L−r_V。\n\nK 的已量化范围是 [0,n_K)，V 的已量化范围是 [0,n_V)，通常不同。例如 G=2、R=4、L=8 时，K 已全部量化，V 的后四行仍为高精度。不能按 K 的分区边界切出 V 的概率权重。\n\n```pseudocode\n输入：私有状态、当前层新 K_t/V_t、G、R、通道数 d、量化合同\n状态初始化：L←0；K码/参数表、V码/参数表为空；Kr、Vr为空\n           K起点←0；V起点←0\n输出：长度 L+1 的新状态候选\n\n核对新行宽度、身份与位置 t=L\n将 K_t 追加到 Kr，将 V_t 追加到 Vr\n若 len(Kr)=R：\n    对偏移 u=0,G,…,R−G：\n        对每个 channel c：\n            量化 Kr[u:u+G,c]\n            存入参数身份 (floor((K起点+u)/G),c) 对应的码与参数\n    K起点←K起点+R；清空 Kr\n若 len(Vr)>R：\n    取最旧一行，其逻辑位置为 V起点\n    对 h=0…ceil(d/G)−1：\n        量化该行 [hG:min((h+1)G,d)]，不让填充参与范围归约\n        存入参数身份 (V起点,h) 对应的码与参数\n    删除 Vr 最旧行；V起点←V起点+1\nL←L+1\n等待全部码、参数、残差与描述完成后，才允许发布该候选\n```\n\n批量 prefill 可以按上述范围建立缓存，但论文强调当前 prefill attention 使用高精度 K/V，低位缓存供后续 decode 消费。逐 token 运行近似 attention 处理整个 prompt 是另一种算法，不能把它与该 prefill 路径混为一谈。L=0 时输出空缓存；L<R 时两侧都没有量化组；d 不能整除 G 时，V 的末通道组只包含有效通道。\n\n解码按逻辑位置独立定位 K、V。得到全部 logits 后做整体 softmax，再按 V 自己的边界消费 p[0:n_V) 与 p[n_V:L)。也可直接使用下面的在线归约，避免完整物化 logits 和高精度 KV。\n\n```pseudocode\n输入：q、合法逻辑集合 A、K/V 的独立码表与残差边界\n输出：attention 输出和可选的逐位置概率统计\n\nm←未设置；质量 l←0；加权和 U←零向量\n对 A 中每个逻辑位置 j，按不重复、不漏项的 tile 遍历：\n    按 K 的组身份或 Kr 读取并重建 K_j\n    按 V 的 token/通道组身份或 Vr 读取并重建 V_j\n    z←a·q·K_j＋位置偏置\n    若 l=0：m←z；l←1；U←V_j\n    否则：\n        m新←max(m,z)；r←exp(m−m新)；e←exp(z−m新)\n        U←r·U＋e·V_j；l←r·l＋e；m←m新\n若 l=0：输出零并标记为空\n否则：输出 U/l；如需评分，再由最终 m、l 恢复各位置概率\n```\n\n分别对量化区与残差区 softmax 后直接相加是错误函数。三个等分数位置的 V 为 [0,2,4]，整体输出是 2；分成 [0] 和 [2,4] 后各自归一化再相加得到 3。K/V 边界不同还意味着即使完成整体 softmax，也必须让每个概率与同一逻辑位置的 V 配对。\n\n## 从码到访问路径与条件化成本\n\n一个可检查的示例格式可以将 w-bit 无符号码按 token-major 顺序组成连续位流。紧密存放时，元素 (j,c) 的位偏移为 w(jd+c)，字节位置为其除以 8 的商，字节内偏移为余数；跨字节码须合并相邻字节再掩码。分页、逐行补齐或稀疏压紧后，先由逻辑位置得到实际码槽，再计算位地址。参数仍按原逻辑组身份查找，不能用物理页号或压紧后的槽号替代。\n\n消费者的完整路径是：固定可读快照 → 取得逻辑位置及 head 映射 → 定位码/残差 → 取得对应冻结参数 → 解包并恢复数值 → 计算 QK、整体归约及 PV → 等待最后读取结束后复用缓冲。tile 内解量化可将高精度片段限制在寄存器或片上暂存；完整展开则先写出全部高精度 KV，再由 attention 读取。前者可能重复解包，后者新增完整副本及写读交通。这里不指定某个 GPU 指令、线程布局或事务合并能力，实际硬件路径需要依据目标内核合同验证。\n\n设每组 scale 与偏移合计 m 字节、高精度元素 b_H 字节，忽略对齐，采用独立的紧密 K/V 位流，则单层单 KV head 的容量为\n\nB_code=ceil(n_K d w/8)＋ceil(n_V d w/8)，\n\nB_meta=m[(n_K/G)d＋n_V ceil(d/G)]，\n\nB_res=b_H d(r_K＋r_V)。\n\n一组 G 个有效值的理想有效位宽为 w＋8m/G。V 的短末组、逐行/逐组字节对齐、索引和版本头会增加它。全模型再按实际物理 head 副本和层求和；GQA/TP 复制不能按唯一逻辑 head 数直接省略。\n\n保留原设定：FP16 KV 共 2 GiB、4096 token，假设两侧各保留 128 个高精度 token，其余为 2 bit、G=32、m=4 字节。高精度为 64 MiB，码为 248 MiB，参数为 124 MiB，合计 436 MiB，约 21.3%。这是两侧都按 128 行残差计算的容量上界。按本节顺序追加后的精确边界，L=4096、R=128 时 r_K=0、r_V=128，对应码 252 MiB、参数 126 MiB、残差 32 MiB，合计 410 MiB。两个数采用不同残差条件，不能互相替代；均未包括工作区，也不声明质量达标。\n\n量化一批 R 行 K 需要 O(Rd) 范围归约与编码，平均每新增 token 为 O(d)，但封组步会出现突发工作。V 稳态每步量化一行，也是 O(d)。attention 对 S 个实际可见位置的 QK/PV 主项约 4Sd FLOP，另有 O(S) 指数/归约和 O(Sd) 解量化操作。位宽下降主要减少候选读取字节，不按比例消除乘加。\n\n| 成本 | 必须计入的对象 |\n|---|---|\n| 计算 | min/max、编码、解包、尺度应用、attention、评分与选择 |\n| 访存 | 码与参数读取、残差、统计数组、gather、展开副本的写读 |\n| 通信 | 实际传输的码、参数、头、概率表、位置表及物理副本 |\n| 准备 | 格式转换、布局建立、预编码、概率统计和控制器校准 |\n| 同步 | 封组完成、码与参数共同可见、统计完成、最后读者结束 |\n| 驻留 | 码、参数、残差、旧快照、私有候选、工作区及双缓冲 |\n\n峰值沿同时存活对象求和。被旧读者持有的版本不能提前扣除。存在重叠时沿依赖图和竞争资源估计关键路径，不能把全部阶段简单相加，也不能默认解码、传输和 attention 完全重叠。\n\n## H2O 式保留：预算、初始化与提交\n\n[H2O v1](../sources/S-H2O.md) §3–4 使用累计 attention 和近期 token。明确总预算 B=B_H＋B_R，B_H、B_R 为非负整数。论文 §5.1 均分两区，附录 A 用 k 分别表示两区，因此实际总槽数为 2k。百分比必须说明分母：prompt 1000 token、总预算 200，生成至总长 1500 后仍只占当前历史约 13.3%。\n\n一种明确的初始化是先对 prompt 完成全历史 causal prefill，逐层/head 累计当时实际计算的归一化 attention，随后保留最近 B_R 行和其余位置中最高分的至多 B_H 行。空 prompt 的统计和集合为空，但这不自动提供生成所需的合法起始输入。若改成 prompt 内逐行淘汰，后续 prompt 行已经读取近似历史，应作为另一配置重新评估。\n\n下面固定单 token 追加、预算不变、同分优先较小逻辑位置的执行约定。F 只需保留仍存活位置的累计分数，删除后不再追踪无法读取的真实未来贡献。\n\n```pseudocode\n输入：旧集合 S、旧累计分数 F、位置 t 的新 K/V、B_H、B_R\n前提：|S|≤B_H+B_R；S 中的状态已完成；近期历史仍在 S 中\n输出：本步输出、新集合与新分数的私有候选\n\nA←S∪{t}；新 K/V 先置于独立 staging\n对 A 读取旧状态和 staging，整体 softmax，得到 o_t 与 p_t,j\n对 j∈A：F候选[j]←F.get(j,0)＋p_t,j\nR←最近 min(B_R,t+1) 个逻辑位置；核对 R⊆A\nC←A\\R；H←空集\n重复至多 min(B_H,|C|) 次：\n    扫描尚未选择的候选，选 F候选 最大者，同分选较小逻辑位置\n    将其加入 H\nS新←R∪H；F新仅保留 S新 对应条目\n核对 |S新|≤B_H+B_R\n本步输出、评分快照和所有旧读取结束后，才允许覆盖淘汰槽\n共同发布 S新、F新、逻辑位置映射、内容和槽代次\n```\n\n持久集合至多 B 行，当前步的 A 可能有 B＋1 行，staging 及旧读者还增加瞬时驻留。近期区的环形物理顺序不等于逻辑顺序；复用槽必须同时更新位置、内容、分数和代次。选择错误和提前覆盖是不同问题：即使 S新 正确，先覆盖旧槽再计算本步贡献仍会读错。\n\n上述重复扫描为 O(B·B_H)，全排序可改为 O(B log B)。在固定预算、一次只新增一行且旧集合已满足条件时，可组织成一次淘汰扫描，但必须保持评分与近期保护规则。许多融合 attention kernel 只返回输出和 LSE，不能据此直接获得每个历史位置的 p。额外统计路径可能需要暂存或第二遍 QK；这一成本不能省略。\n\n累计分数偏向有更多累加机会的旧 token。论文附录 B.2 报告平均分替代在其测试中退化，这既不证明累计分对所有任务最优，也不允许把换成平均分称作同一算法。在线策略只能利用已经观察的贡献，不能借用未来生成作为可部署选择器。\n\nGQA/MQA 中，多个 query head 共享一个 KV head。可对对应 head 的分数求和或平均后共享选择；也可独立保存各自集合；或保留并集。若 B=3、近期位置为 3，两个 query head 分别选择 {0,1,3}、{1,2,3}，并集有四行，不能继续按三行计容量。独立副本则可能损失原有共享收益。这些组合需要新的质量与资源对照。\n\n## 删除的误差、反例与证据范围\n\n固定同一组完整历史的分数与 V，设删除集合 D 的概率质量 δ=Σ_{j∈D}p_j，保留集合非空且 δ<1。删除后重新归一化，输出差为\n\no−o_S=δ(μ_D−μ_S)，\n\n其中 μ_D、μ_S 是各集合按原概率重新归一化的加权均值。若 ||V_j||≤M，则 ||o−o_S||≤2Mδ。这个局部界还没有包含后续层和生成轨迹变化；累计历史评分也不保证下一步 δ 小。\n\nH2O §3.1 的稀疏性按每行最大 attention 的 1% 阈值统计，并非严格零。许多小项可以累积出大质量，大幅值 V 可以放大输出差。长时间低关注后突然查询早期实体或数字、话题切换、多轮追加、不同 head 依赖不同位置，都是必要反例。删除位置以后，扩大预算不能仅靠改长度恢复它，必须有合法副本或重算路径。\n\n质量对照分别运行完整缓存、仅近期、仅重要和组合，并固定实际总预算。参考须实际消费各自历史；仅改变 mask、仍保留完整物理缓存可以研究近似输出，却不能证明容量收益。分支回滚、环形绕回和异步槽覆盖另查状态正确性。\n\n性能证据仍受设置约束。H2O §5.2 使用等长 padding 的合成输入和固定输出长度；generation throughput 的分子为生成 token 数，分母包含 prompt 与 decode 时间。部分长上下文速度测试超出 OPT 原训练上下文，不能作为该长度长期质量证据。附录 C.2 的额外吞吐来自 FlexGen 权重压缩，不能读成任意 KV 量化与保留组合已经通过。真实服务还需到达过程、长度长尾、排队及相同质量约束。\n\n## CacheGen 式传输：锚点、量化码与熵区间\n\n[CacheGen v1](../sources/S-CACHEGEN.md) §4 将完整 KV 张量变成可传输位流。每个 chunk 的首 token 为锚点，先量化并重建 Â；其余 token 编码 Δ_j=F_j−Â，再量化 delta。解码使用同一个 Â，得到 F̂_j=Â＋Δ̂_j。若编码端减原始锚点 A，解码端却加 Â，会额外引入 Â−A 的偏移。\n\n固定 v1 采用十 token chunk、锚点 8 bit，以及按浅/中/深层选择 delta 位宽的若干等级。这些是论文配置，不是通用最佳值。与链式相邻差分相比，共用锚点允许 chunk 内 delta 并行恢复；锚点精度和 chunk 长度又影响 delta 分布、开销和质量。\n\n为说明熵编码本身，设一个独立符号流使用固定整数频数 f_s>0，总数 M，累计频数 C_s=Σ_{u<s}f_u。算术编码将符号序列缩成嵌套区间。下面是精确有理数解释模型，足以重建机制；生产实现通常使用有限宽整数区间与重整化，须另核对舍入、终止和格式兼容，不能直接用此模型推算吞吐。\n\n```pseudocode\n输入：量化整数符号序列、正频数表 f、符号数 N\n输出：区间内的有限二进制标签及其位数\n\n编码：\n    low←0；high←1\n    对每个符号 s：\n        width←high−low；oldlow←low\n        low←oldlow＋width·C_s/M\n        high←oldlow＋width·(C_s+f_s)/M\n    从 n=0 起递增：k←ceil(low·2^n)\n    首次满足 k/2^n<high 时，保存 k 的 n 位标签和 N，结束\n\n解码：\n    x←k/2^n；核对 0≤x<1，N 与格式一致\n    重复 N 次：\n        找唯一 s，使 C_s/M≤x<(C_s+f_s)/M\n        输出 s；x←(M·x−\n\n[EXCERPT extracted-character range 12000:15488]\n轴。\n\n## 检查、竞争解释与未测边界\n\n检查顺序是原模型参考、同一码与冻结参数的独立解码参考、实际消费者。第一与第二比较近似损失，第二与第三定位执行错误。固定前缀先定位首次分歧，再另测真实自回归和任务质量；不能在两路已经生成不同 token 后，把所有后续差异归为同输入 kernel 误差。\n\n随附 tools/check_k_alg_021_full.py 为确定性、只读、离线的标准库软件检查。量化使用 Fraction 和枚举网格参考，覆盖有符号偏移、常数组、空输入、不同 G/R、短通道尾组和逐次封组；空输入入口只返回一个状态字典，不再把字典解包成两个返回值。attention 使用独立整行公式检查在线归约及不同 K/V 边界。H2O 用子集枚举验证选择和预算，并实际构造提前覆盖及 GQA 并集超预算。传输部分执行小型有理数区间编解码、锚点恢复和带单位的成本比较。\n\n| 观察 | 竞争解释 | 区分实验与反证后的修正 |\n|---|---|---|\n| 同一码重建值错误 | signed 偏移、组轴、保存参数或码槽映射错 | 枚举最近网格，分别改变一组参数与物理排列；失败先修制品执行 |\n| 只在封组后失败 | K/V 边界混用、尺度发布或尾组问题 | 跨 R−1、R、R+1 连续消费；逐位置对照两侧边界 |\n| 各分区输出合理，总输出错误 | 独立归一化、漏项或重复项 | 不等长分区加空分区，与整行 softmax 比较；拒绝直接相加 |\n| 集合正确但本步输出错 | 提前覆盖旧槽或读者未结束 | 保持选择不变，只延迟写回；实际读取旧槽内容核验 |\n| 长距离问题退化 | 冷门 token 被删、量化或任务漂移 | 分开启用量化与保留，再恢复早期 token；只在对应干预修复后支持归因 |\n| GQA 仍报 B 行但容量扩大 | 并集或物理副本未计 | 枚举各 head 集合及物理并集，修正预算或选择策略 |\n| 位流解出整数但状态错误 | 错表、错误锚点、位置/前缀身份错误 | 对照整数序列与独立重建；分别注入表错和原始锚点差分 |\n| 压缩在高带宽反而慢 | 解码、转换、CPU 排队或预测单位错误 | 扫带宽，固定质量与计时边界；对照临界点并修正在线成本 |\n| 字节减少但完整时间不降 | 事务未降、解包、统计、同步或排队主导 | 分开记录逻辑字节、设备事务、kernel 与完整调用，撤回未经支持的带宽解释 |\n\n负对照必须实际进入被检路径。两份代码复制同一 min/max 公式不构成独立参考；只打印“发现超预算”而没有断言也不构成检测。算术编码往返不能证明损坏检测能力，软件发布次序不能证明 GPU 可见性。\n\n本章检查不执行目标 CPU/GPU kernel，不模拟完整低精度舍入、有限宽算术编码器、真实分页异步协议或多卡通信，不测设备事务、网络吞吐、任务质量或 Agent 使用效果。Python 的真实执行状态由本轮执行记录说明，不由正文预先宣称。设备与服务实验仍须固定模型、制品、输入/输出分布、质量阈值、实际后端、预热及计时边界，报告转换、统计、同步、峰值和失败样本。\n\n<!-- infraagents:provenance -->\n\n## 来源与推导\n\n- [S-KIVI：KIVI](../sources/S-KIVI.md)：固定 2402.02750v1 的 §3、附录 "7dfb3a43ba73d9d9861c06311005edf12dfafa9f60a268fec327deb4d8e8770d", "reading_scope": "full", "category": "source_original", "content": "--- PAGE 1 ---\nSGLang: Efficient Execution of\nStructured Language Model Programs\nLianmin Zheng2∗\nLiangsheng Yin3\nZhiqiang Xie1\nChuyue Sun1\nJeff Huang4\nCody Hao Yu5\nShiyi Cao2\nChristos Kozyrakis1\nIon Stoica2\nJoseph E. Gonzalez2\nClark Barrett1\nYing Sheng1∗\n1 Stanford University\n2 UC Berkeley\n3 Shanghai Jiao Tong University\n4 Texas A&M University\n5 Independent Researcher\nAbstract\nLarge language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for complex tasks that\nrequire multiple generation calls, advanced prompting techniques, control flow, and\nstructured inputs/outputs. However, efficient systems are lacking for programming\nand executing these applications. We introduce SGLang, a system for efficient\nexecution of complex language model programs. SGLang consists of a frontend\nlanguage and a runtime. The frontend simplifies programming with primitives for\ngeneration and parallelism control. The runtime accelerates execution with novel\noptimizations like RadixAttention for KV cache reuse and compressed finite state\nmachines for faster structured output decoding. Experiments show that SGLang\nachieves up to 6.4× higher throughput compared to state-of-the-art inference\nsystems on various large language and multi-modal models on tasks including\nagent control, logical reasoning, few-shot learning benchmarks, JSON decoding,\nretrieval-augmented generation pipelines, and multi-turn chat. The code is publicly\navailable at https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang.\n1\nIntroduction\nRecent increases in the capabilities of LLMs have broadened their utility, enabling them to tackle a\nwider range of general tasks and act as autonomous agents [35, 6, 36, 52, 46]. In such applications,\nLLMs engage in multi-round planning, reasoning, and interaction with external environments. This\nis accomplished through tool usage [41, 38], multiple input modalities [47, 2], and a wide range of\nprompting techniques [30], like few-shot learning [5], self-consistency [53], skeleton-of-thought [33],\nand tree-of-thought [56]. All of these new use cases require multiple, often dependent, LLM\ngeneration calls, showing a trend of using multi-call structures to complete complex tasks [57, 21].\nThe emergence of these patterns signifies a shift in our interaction with LLMs, moving from simple\nchatting to a more sophisticated form of programmatic usage of LLMs, which means using a program\nto schedule and control the generation processes of LLMs. We refer to these programs as \"Language\nModel Programs\" (LM Programs) [4, 20]. The advanced prompting techniques and agentic workflow\nmentioned above fall within the scope of LM programs. There are two common properties of LM\nprograms: (1) LM programs typically contain multiple LLM calls interspersed with control flow.\nThis is needed to complete complex tasks and improve overall quality. (2) LM programs receive\nstructured inputs and produce structured outputs. This is needed to enable the composition of LM\nprograms and to integrate LM programs into existing software systems.\n∗Equal contribution.\nPreprint. Under review.\narXiv:2312.07104v2  [cs.AI]  6 Jun 2024\n\n\n--- PAGE 2 ---\nInterpreter\nSGLang Client (Frontend)\nLanguage primitives (Sec. 2)\nSGLang Runtime (Backend)\nOptimizations: RadixAttention (Sec. 3), Compressed finite \nstate machines (Sec. 4), API speculative execution (Sec. 5)\nFigure 1: System architecture: An interpreter executes language primitives with optimized runtime.\nDespite the widespread use of LM programs, current systems for expressing and executing them\nremain inefficient. We identify two primary challenges associated with the efficient use of LM pro-\ngrams: First, programming LM programs is tedious and difficult due to the non-deterministic nature of\nLLMs. Developing an LM program often requires extensive string manipulation, experimental tuning\nof prompts, brittle output parsing, handling multiple input modalities, and implementing parallelism\nmechanisms. This complexity significantly reduces the readability of even simple programs (Sec. 2).\nSecondly and importantly, executing LM programs is inefficient due to redundant computation\nand memory usage. State-of-the-art inference engines (e.g., vLLM [23], TGI [16], and TensorRT-\nLLM [34]), have been optimized to reduce latency and improve throughput without direct knowledge\nof the workload. This makes these systems general and robust but also results in significant ineffi-\nciencies for any given workload. A prominent example is the reuse of the Key-Value (KV) cache\n(Sec. 3). The KV cache consists of reusable intermediate tensors that are essential for generative\ninference. During typical batch executions of LM programs, numerous opportunities exist to reuse\nthe KV cache across multiple different LLM calls that share a common prefix. However, current\nsystems lack effective mechanisms to facilitate this reuse, resulting in unnecessary computations and\nwasted memory. Another example is constrained decoding for structured outputs (e.g., JSON mode),\nwhere the output of LLMs is restricted to follow specific grammatical rules defined by a regular\nexpression (Sec. 4). Under these constraints, multiple tokens can often be decoded once. However,\nexisting systems only decode one token at a time, leading to suboptimal decoding speeds.\nTo address these challenges, we present SGLang, a Structured Generation Language for LLMs. The\ncore idea is to systematically exploit the multi-call structure in LM programs for efficient execution.\nAs shown in Fig. 1, it has two parts: a front-end language and a back-end runtime. The front-end\nsimplifies the programming of LM programs, and the runtime accelerates their execution. The two\nparts can work together for better performance but can also function independently.\nWe introduce SGLang as a domain-specific language embedded in Python. It provides primitives\nfor generation (e.g., extend, gen, select) and parallelism control (e.g., fork, join). SGLang\nis compatible with Python’s control flow and libraries, so users can develop advanced prompting\nworkflows easily with native Python syntax. We provide an interpreter and a compiler for SGLang.\nThe interpreter manages the prompt state as a stream and submits primitive operations to the stream for\nasynchronous execution, ensuring proper control over synchronization and intra-program parallelism.\nAdditionally, SGLang program can be traced and compiled for more optimizations.\nOn the runtime side, we propose several novel optimizations to accelerate the execution of SGLang\nprograms. The first technique, RadixAttention, enables the automatic reuse of the KV cache across\nmultiple generation calls. In existing inference engines, the KV cache of a request is discarded\nafter processing is completed, preventing the KV cache from being reused across multiple calls and\nsignificantly slowing down the execution. Instead, our system maintains an LRU cache of the KV\ncache for all requests within a radix tree. This approach manages the KV cache as a traditional cache\nand uses a radix tree for efficient matching, insertion, and eviction. It allows the runtime to handle\nvarious reuse patterns with a cache-aware scheduling policy efficiently. The second technique is a\ncompressed finite state machine, which enables faster constrained decoding for structured outputs.\nExisting systems follow the constraints only for the next token by masking probabilities of disallowed\ntokens, making them able to decode only one token at a time. Instead, our system analyzes the\nconstraints and builds a compressed finite-state machine to represent the constraint. This approach\ncompresses a multi-token path into a single-step path whenever possible, allowing the decoding of\nmultiple tokens at once to achieve faster decoding speed. Lastly, SGLang also supports API-only\nmodels like OpenAI’s GPT-4, and we introduce the third technique, API speculative execution, to\noptimize multi-call programs for API-only models.\nUsing SGLang, we implemented various LLM applications, including agent control, logical reasoning,\nfew-shot learning benchmarks, JSON decoding, retrieval-augmented generation pipelines, multi-\nturn chat, and multi-modality processing. We tested the performance on models including Llama-\n7B/70B [49], Mistral-8x7B [17], LLaVA-v1.5-7B (image) [28], and LLaVA-NeXT-34B (video) [62]\non NVIDIA A10G and A100 GPUs. Experimental results show that SGLang achieves up to 6.4×\nhigher throughput across a wide range of workloads, models, and hardware setups, compared to\nexisting programming and inference systems, including Guidance [13], vLLM [23], and LMQL [4].\n2\n\n\n--- PAGE 3 ---\ndimensions = [\"Clarity\", \"Originality\", \"Evidence\"]\n@function\ndef multi_dimensional_judge(s, path, essay): \ns += system(\"Evaluate an essay about an image.\")\ns += user(image(path) + \"Essay:\" + essay)\ns += assistant(\"Sure!\")\n# Return directly if it is not related\ns += user(\"Is the essay related to the image?\")\ns += assistant(select(\"related\", choices=[\"yes\", \"no\"]))\nif s[\"related\"] == \"no\": return\n# Judge multiple dimensions in parallel\nforks = s.fork(len(dimensions))\nfor f, dim in zip(forks, dimensions):\nf += user(\"Evaluate based on the following dimension:\" +\ndim + \". End your judgment with the word 'END'\")\nf += assistant(\"Judgment:\" + gen(\"judgment\", stop=\"END\"))\n# Merge the judgments\njudgment = \"\\n\".join(f[\"judgment\"] for f in forks)\n# Generate a summary and a grade. Primitives provided by SGLang are shown in red.\n2\nProgramming Model\nThis section introduces the SGLang programming model with a running example, describes its\nlanguage primitives and execution modes, and outlines runtime optimization opportunities. This\nprogramming model can simplify tedious operations in multi-call workflows (e.g., string manipulation,\nAPI calling, constraint specification, parallelism) by providing flexible and composable primitives.\nA running example. The language is a domain-specific language embedded in Python. Fig. 2 shows a\nprogram that evaluates an essay about an image using the branch-solve-merge prompting method [40].\nThe function multi_dimensional_judge takes three arguments: s, path, and essay. s manages\nthe prompt state, path is the image file path, and essay is the essay text. New strings and SGLang\nprimitives can be appended to the state s for execution using the += operator. First, the function\nadds the image and essay to the prompt. It then checks if the essay is related to the image using\nselect, storing the result in s[\"related\"]. If related, the prompt is forked into three copies for\nparallel evaluation from different dimensions, using gen to store results in f[\"judgment\"]. Next, it\nmerges the judgments, generates a summary, and assigns a letter grade. Finally, it returns the results\nin JSON format, following a schema defined by a regular expression constraint regex. SGLang\ngreatly simplifies this program, as an equivalent program using an OpenAI API-like interface would\ntake 2.1× as many lines of code due to manual string manipulation and parallelism control.\nLanguage primitives. SGLang provides primitives for controlling prompt state, generation, and\nparallelism. They can be used together with Python syntax and libraries. Here are the primitives:\n“gen” calls a model to generate and stores the results in a variable with the name specified in its first\nargument. It supports a “regex” argument to constrain the output to follow a grammar defined by a\nregular expression (e.g., a JSON schema). “select” calls a model to choose the highest probability\noption from a list. The operator “+=” or “extend” appends a string to the prompt. The operator\n“[variable_name]” fetches the results of a generation. “fork” creates parallel forks of the prompt\nstate. “join” rejoins the prompt state. “image” and “video” take in image and video inputs.\nExecution modes. The simplest way to execute an SGLang program is through an interpreter, where a\nprompt is treated as an asynchronous stream. Primitives like extend, gen, and select are submitted\nto the stream for asynchronous execution. These non-blocking calls allow Python code to continue\nrunning without waiting for the generation to finish. This is similar to launching CUDA kernels\nasynchronously. Each prompt is managed by a stream executor in a background thread, enabling\nintra-program parallelism. Fetching generation results will block until they are ready, ensuring correct\nsynchronization. Alternatively, SGLang programs can be compiled as computational graphs and\n3\n\n\n--- PAGE 4 ---\nTable 1: Comparison among LMQL, Guidance, and SGLang.\nSystem\nSyntax\nLanguage Primitives\nRuntime Backends\nLMQL\nCustom\nextend, gen, select\nHF Transformers, llama.cpp, OpenAI\nGuidance\nPython\nextend, gen, select, image\nHF Transformers, llama.cpp, OpenAI\nSGLang\nPython\nextend, gen, select, image, video, fork, join\nSGLang Runtime (SRT), OpenAI\nexecuted with a graph executor, allowing for more optimizations. This paper uses interpreter mode by\ndefault and discusses compiler mode results in Appendix D. SGLang supports open-weight models\nwith its own SGLang Runtime (SRT), as well as API models such as OpenAI and Anthropic models.\nComparison. Programming systems for LLMs can be classified as high-level (e.g., LangChain,\nDSPy) and low-level (e.g., LMQL, Guidance, SGLang). High-level systems provide predefined or\nauto-generated prompts, such as DSPy’s prompt optimizer. Low-level systems typically do not alter\nprompts but allow direct manipulation of prompts and primitives. SGLang is a low-level system\nsimilar to LMQL and Guidance. Table 1 compares their features. SGLang focuses more on runtime\nefficiency and comes with its own co-designed runtime, allowing for novel optimizations introduced\nlater. High-level languages (e.g., DSPy) can be compiled to low-level languages (e.g., SGLang). We\ndemonstrate the integration of SGLang as a backend in DSPy for better runtime efficiency in Sec. 6.\nRuntime optimizations. Fig. 2 shows three runtime optimization opportunities: KV cache reuse,\nfast constrained decoding, API speculative execution. We will discuss them in the following sections.\n3\nEfficient KV Cache Reuse with RadixAttention\nSGLang programs can chain multiple generation calls and create parallel copies with the \"fork\"\nprimitive. Additionally, different program instances often share some common parts (e.g., system\nprompts). These scenarios create many shared prompt prefixes during execution, leading to numerous\nopportunities for reusing the KV cache. During LLM inference, the KV cache stores intermediate\ntensors from the forward pass, reused for decoding future tokens. They are named after key-value\npairs in the self-attention mechanism [51]. KV cache computation depends only on prefix tokens.\nTherefore, requests with the same prompt prefix can reuse the KV cache, reducing redundant\ncomputation and memory usage. More background and some examples are provided in Appendix A.\nGiven the KV cache reuse opportunity, a key challenge in optimizing SGLang programs is reusing the\nKV cache across multiple calls and instances. While some systems explore certain KV cache reuse\ncases [23, 58, 18, 12], they often need manual configurations and cannot handle all reuse patterns\n(e.g., dynamic tree structures). Consequently, most state-of-the-art inference systems recompute the\nKV cache for each request. We will discuss their limitations and our differences in Sec. 7.\nThis section introduces RadixAttention, a novel technique for automatic and systematic KV cache\nreuse during runtime. Unlike existing systems that discard the KV cache after a generation request\nfinishes, our system retains the cache for prompts and generation results in a radix tree, enabling\nefficient prefix search, reuse, insertion, and eviction. We implement an LRU eviction policy and\na cache-aware scheduling policy to enhance the cache hit rate. RadixAttention is compatible with\ntechniques like continuous batching [60], paged attention [23], and tensor parallelism [44]. In\naddition, it introduces only negligible memory and time overhead when there is no cache hit.\nRadixAttention. A radix tree is a data structure that serves as a space-efficient alternative to a\nclassical trie (prefix tree). Unlike typical trees, the edges of a radix tree can be labeled not just\nwith single elements but also with sequences of elements of varying lengths, significantly enhancing\nefficiency. In our system, we utilize a radix tree to manage a mapping between sequences of tokens,\nand their corresponding KV cache tensors. These KV cache tensors are stored in a non-contiguous,\npaged layout, where the size of each page is equivalent to one token. Because GPU memory is quickly\nfilled by the KV cahce, we introduce a simple LRU eviction policy that evicts the least recently\nused leaf first. By evicting leaves first, we enable the re-use of their common ancestors until those\nancestors become leaves and are also evicted.\nIn the continuous batching setting, we cannot evict nodes used by the currently running batch.\nTherefore, each node maintains a reference counter indicating how many running requests are using\nit. A node is evictable if its reference counter is zero. Note that we do not preallocate a fixed-size\nmemory pool as a cache. Instead, we let the cached tokens and the currently running requests share\nthe same memory pool. Therefore, the system dynamically allocates memory for cache and running\nrequests. When enough waiting requests run, the system will evict all cached tokens in favor of a\nlarger batch size. Fig. 3 shows how the radix tree is maintained for several incoming requests. The\n4\n\n\n--- PAGE 5 ---\n(1)\nYou are a helpful assistant.\nUser: Hello!\nAssistant: Hi!\na\n(2)\nYou are a helpful assistant.\nUser: Hello!\nAssistant: Hi!\nb\nUser: Solve this problem …\nAssistant: Sure! …\n(3)\nc\nd\nYou are a helpful assistant.\nUser: Hello!\nAssistant: Hi!\nUser: What can you do?\nAssistant: I can ...\nUser: Solve this question…\nAssistant: Sure! …\n(4)\nX\nYou are a helpful assistant.\nUser: Hello!\nAssistant: Hi!\nUser: What can you do?\nAssistant: I can ...\n(5)\nevicted\nUser: Write a story …\nAssistant: Sure! …\nYou are a helpful assistant.\nUser: Hello!\nAssistant: Hi!\nUser: What can you do?\nAssistant: I can ...\n(6)\nUser: Write a story …\nAssistant: Sure! …\ne\nQuestion 1: …\nAnswer 1: …\nQuestion 2: ...\nAnswer 2:…\nQuestion 3: ..\nAnswer 3: ..\nf\ng\nYou are a helpful assistant.\nUser: Hello!\nAssistant: Hi!\nUser: What can you do?\nAssistant: I can ...\n(7)\nh\nUser: Write a story …\nAssistant: Sure! …\nQuestion 1: …\nAnswer 1: …\nQuestion 2: ...\nAnswer 2:…\nQuestion 3:\nWhat …\nAnswer 3: ...\nWhen ...\nAnswer 3: ...\nHow …\nAnswer 3: ...\nX\nYou are a helpful assistant.\nUser: Hello!\nAssistant: Hi!\n(8)\nX\nQuestion 1: …\nAnswer 1: …\nQuestion 2: ...\nAnswer 2:…\nQuestion 3:\nj\nWhat …\nAnswer 3: ...\nk\nWhen ...\nAnswer 3: ...\nHow …\nAnswer 3: ...\nl\ni\nUser: Solve this question…\nAssistant: Sure! …\nUser: How about ..?\nAssistant: It is a …\nevicted\nYou are a helpful assistant.\nUser: Hello!\nAssistant: Hi!\n(9)\nQuestion 1: …\nAnswer 1: …\nQuestion 2: ...\nAnswer 2:…\nQuestion 3:\nWhat …\nAnswer 3:\nX\nevicted\nX\nX\nevicted\nThis is ...\nLet us ...\nWe can ...\nTo solve …\nFigure 3: Examples of RadixAttention operations with an LRU eviction policy, illustrated across\nnine time points. The figure demonstrates the dynamic evolution of the radix tree in response to\nvarious requests. These requests include two chat sessions, a batch of few-shot learning inquiries,\nand a self-consistency sampling. Each tree edge carries a label denoting a substring or a sequence of\ntokens. The nodes are color-coded to reflect different states: green for newly added nodes, blue for\ncached nodes accessed during the time point, and red for nodes that have been evicted. In step (1),\nthe radix tree is initially empty. In step (2), the server processes an incoming user message \"Hello\"\nand responds with the LLM output \"Hi\". The system prompt \"You are a helpful assistant\", the user\nmessage \"Hello!\", and the LLM reply \"Hi!\" are consolidated into the tree as a single edge linked to a\nnew node. In step (3), a new prompt arrives and the server finds the prefix of the prompt (i.e., the first\nturn of the conversation) in the radix tree and reuses its KV cache. The new turn is appended to the\ntree as a new node. In step (4), a new chat session begins. The node “b” from (3) is split into two\nnodes to allow the two chat sessions to share the system prompt. In step (5), the second chat session\ncontinues. However, due to the memory limit, node \"c\" from (4) must be evicted. The new turn is\nappended after node \"d\" in (4). In step (6), the server receives a few-shot learning query, processes it,\nand inserts it into the tree. The root node is split because the new query does not share any prefix with\nexisting nodes. In step (7), the server receives a batch of additional few-shot learning queries. These\nqueries share the same set of few-shot examples, so we split node ’e’ from (6) to enable sharing. In\nstep (8), the server receives a new message from the first chat session. It evicts all nodes from the\nsecond chat session (node \"g\" and \"h\") as they are least recently used. In step (9), the server receives\na request to sample more answers for the questions in node \"j\" from (8), likely for self-consistency\nprompting. To make space for these requests, we evict node \"i\", \"k\", and \"l\" in (8).\nfrontend interpreter sends full prompts to the runtime, and the runtime performs prefix matching and\nreuse. The tree structure is stored on the CPU with negligible maintenance overhead. During the\nexecution of the fork primitive, the frontend sends the prefix first as a hint, ensuring the prefix is\ncorrectly inserted into the tree. It then sends the remaining prompts. This \"Frontend Hint\" simplifies\nruntime scheduling and matching, exemplifying the benefits of frontend-runtime co-design.\nCache-aware scheduling. We define the cache hit rate as number of cached prompt tokens\nnumber of prompt tokens\n. When there are\nmany requests in the waiting queue, the order in which they are executed can significantly impact\nthe cache hit rate. For example, if the request scheduler frequently switches between different,\nunrelated requests, it can lead to cache thrashing and a low hit rate. We design a cache-aware\nscheduling algorithm to increase the cache hit rate. In the batch-processing setting we sort the\n5\n\n\n--- PAGE 6 ---\nFSM state\nToken\nLLM decode\n(c) Decoding process with normal FSM\n(d) Decoding process with compressed FSM\n(a) Normal FSM for regex {\"summary\": \"\n1\n{\"summary\":  \"\n0\n(b) Compressed FSM for regex {\"summary\": \"\n{\"\nsummary\nLLM\n\":\nLLM\nLLM\n_\"\nLLM\nLLM\n{\"\nsummary\n\":\n_\"\n1\n2\n3\n5\n4\n6\n7\n8\n10\n9\n11\n{\n\"\ns\nu\nm\nm\na\nr\ny\n\"\n:\n0\n12\n13\n\"\n_\nFigure 4: The decoding process of normal and compressed FSMs (the underscore _ means a space).\nrequests by matched prefix length and prioritize requests with longer matched prefixes instead of\nusing a first-come, first-served schedule. Alg. 1 (Appendix) shows the pseudo-code for cache-aware\nscheduling with contiguous batching. The algorithm uses longest-shared-prefix-first order. In more\nlatency-sensitive settings we may still be able to tolerate limited batch re-ordering to improve cache\nreuse. Additionally, we prove the following theorem for optimal scheduling in the offline case. 2\nTheorem 3.1. For a batch of requests, we can achieve an optimal cache hit rate by visiting the radix\ntree of the requests in the depth-first search order, with a cache size ≥the maximum request length.\nThe longest-shared-prefix-first order is equivalent to a depth-first search order.\nThe proof is in Sec. A.3 (Appendix). In the online case, the DFS order will be disrupted, but\nour schedule still approximates the DFS behavior on the augmented part of the full radix tree, as\ndescribed in Sec. A.3. While greedy cache-aware scheduling can achieve high throughput, it can lead\nto starvation. We leave its integration with other fair scheduling methods [42] as future work.\nDistributed Cases. RadixAttention can be extended to multiple GPUs. For tensor parallelism, each\nGPU maintains a sharded KV cache. There is no need for additional synchronization because the tree\noperations are the same. Data parallelism with multiple workers is discussed in Sec. A.4 (Appendix).\n4\nEfficient Constrained Decoding with Compressed Finite State Machine\nIn LM programs, users often want to constrain the model’s output to follow specific formats, such\nas JSON schemas. This can improve controllability and robustness, and make the output easier to\nparse. SGLang offers a regex argument to enforce such constraints using regular expressions, which\nare expressive enough for many practical scenarios. Existing systems support this by converting a\nregular expression into a finite state machine (FSM) [54]. During decoding, they maintain the current\nFSM state, retrieve allowed tokens from the next states, and set the probability of invalid tokens to\nzero, decoding token by token. This token-by-token approach, however, is inefficient when there are\nopportunities to decode multiple tokens at once. For example, the constant sequence {\"summary\":\n\" in Fig. 2 spans multiple tokens in the normal decoding process as shown in Fig. 4 (c), requiring\nmultiple decoding stages, even though there is only one valid next token when decoding it. Therefore,\nthe whole sequence can be decoded in a single step (i.e., forward pass). However, existing systems\ncan only decode one token at a time because the lack of integration between the FSM and the model\nrunner in existing systems prevents multi-token processing, resulting in slow decoding.\nSGLang overcomes this limitation by creating a fast constrained decoding runtime with a compressed\nFSM. This runtime analyzes the FSM and compresses adjacent singular-transition edges in the FSM\ninto single edges as demonstrated in Fig. 4 (b), allowing it to recognize when multiple tokens can be\ndecoded together. In Fig. 4 (d), multiple tokens on the compressed transition edge can be decoded in\none forward pass, which greatly accelerates the decoding process. It is also general and applicable to\nall regular expressions. More details on the background and implementation are in Appendix B.\n5\nEfficient Endpoint Calling with API Speculative Execution\nThe previous sections introduced optimizations for open-weight models, which require modifications\nto the model inference process. Additionally, SGLang works with API-access-only models, such as\nOpenAI’s GPT-4. However, for these models, we can only call a black-box API endpoint.\nThis section introduces a new optimization for black-box API models that accelerates execution\nand reduces the API cost of multi-call SGLang programs using speculative execution. For ex-\nample, a program may ask the model to generate a description of a character with a multi-call\n2In practice, the computation is not the same as what is described in the proof of Theorem 3.1 because the\nunpredictable number of output tokens can cause the recomputation of the KV cache.\n6\n\n\n--- PAGE 7 ---\nMMLU\nReAct\nAgents\nGenerative\nAgents\nTree of\nThought\nSkeleton\nof Thought\nLLM Judge HellaSwag\nJSON\nDecoding\nMulti-Turn\nChat(short)\nMulti-Turn\n Chat(long)\nDSPy RAG\nPipeline\n0.0\n0.2\n0.5\n0.8\n1.0\nThroughput\n(Normalized)\nSGLang\nvLLM\nGuidance\nLMQL\nFigure 5: Normalized throughput on Llama-7B models. Higher is better.\npattern: s += context + \"name:\" + gen(\"name\", stop=\"\\n\") + \"job:\" + gen(\"job\",\nstop=\"\\n\"). Naively, the two gen primitives correspond to two API calls, meaning that the user\nneeds to pay for the input token fee on the context twice. In SGLang, we can enable speculative\nexecution on the first call and let it continue the generation of a few more tokens by ignoring the stop\ncondition. The interpreter keeps the additional generation outputs and matches and reuses them with\nlater primitives. In certain cases, with careful prompt engineering, the model can correctly match the\ntemplate with high accuracy, saving us the latency and input costs of one API call.\n6\nEvaluation\nWe evaluate the performance of SGLang across diverse LLM workloads. Subsequently, we conduct\nablation studies and case studies to demonstrate the effectiveness of specific components. SGLang is\nimplemented in PyTorch [37] with custom CUDA kernels from FlashInfer [59] and Triton [48].\n6.1\nSetup\nModels. We test dense Llama-2 models [49], sparse mixture of experts Mixtral models [17], multi-\nmodal LLaVA image [27] and video models [62], and API model OpenAI’s GPT-3.5. For open-weight\nmodels, the number of parameters ranges from 7 billion to 70 billion, and we use float16 precision.\nHardware. We run most experiments on AWS EC2 G5 instances, which are equipped with NVIDIA\nA10G GPUs (24GB). We run 7B models on a single A10G GPU and larger models on multiple A10G\nGPUs with tensor parallelism [44]. We run some additional experiments on A100G (80GB) GPUs.\nBaselines. We compare SGLang against both high-level programming systems with their respective\nlanguages and default runtimes, as well as low-level inference engines with standard OpenAI-like\nCompletion APIs. Unless otherwise stated, we do not turn on optimizations that will change the\ncomputation results so that all systems compute the same results. The baselines include:\n• Guidance[13], a language for controlling LLMs. We use Guidance v0.1.8 with llama.cpp backend.\n• vLLM [23], a high-throughput inference engine. We use vLLM v0.2.5 and its default API server3.\n• LMQL [4], a query language. We use LMQL v0.7.3 with Hugging Face Transformers backend.\nWorkloads. We test the following: 5-shot MMLU [14] and 20-shot HellaSwag [61] benchmarks.\nWe decode one token for MMLU and use primitive select to select the answer with the highest\nprobability for HellaSwag. For the ReAct agent [57] and generative agents [36], we extract the\ntraces from the original papers and replay them. We use the Tree-of-thought [56] for the GSM-8K\nproblems and Skeleton-of-thought [33] for tip generation. We use LLM judges with the branch-solve-\nmerge [40] technique; JSON decoding with a schema specified by a regular expression; Multi-turn\nchat with 4 turns, where the input of each turn is randomly sampled between 256-512 tokens. Multi-\nturn chat (short) means short output (4-8 tokens) and multi-turn chat (long) means long output\n(256-512 tokens); DSPy retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline [20] in its official example.\nMetrics. We report two performance metrics: throughput and latency. For throughput, we run a\nsufficiently large batch of program instances to compute the maximum throughput, comparing the\nnumber of program instances executed per second (programs per second, p/s). For latency, we execute\na single program at a time without batching and report the average latency for multiple instances.\n6.2\nEnd-to-End Performance\nResults on open-weight models. The latency and throughput results are shown in Fig. 5 and Fig. 6.\nSGLang improves throughput by up to 6.4× and reduces latency by up to 3.7×. These improvements\n3RadixAttention has been partially integrated as an optional experimental feature into the latest version of\nvLLM; therefore, we used an earlier version for comparison.\n7\n\n\n--- PAGE 8 ---\nMMLU\nReAct\nAgents\nGenerative\nAgents\nTree of\nThought\nSkeleton\nof Thought\nLLM Judge HellaSwag\nJSON\nDecoding\nMulti-Turn\nChat(short)\nMulti-Turn\n Chat(long)\nDSPy RAG\nPipeline\n0.0\n0.2\n0.5\n0.8\n1.0\nLatency\n(Normalized)\nSGLang\nvLLM\nGuidance\nLMQL\nFigure 6: Normalized latency on Llama-7B models. Lower is better.\nMMLU\nReAct\nAgents\nGenerative\nAgents\nTree of\nThought\nSkeleton\nof Thought\nLLM Judge HellaSwag\nJSON\nDecoding\nMulti-Turn\nChat(short)\nMulti-Turn\n Chat(long)\nDSPy RAG\nPipeline\n0.0\n0.2\n0.5\n0.8\n1.0\nThroughput\n(Normalized)\nSGLang\nvLLM\nFigure 7: Normalized throughput on Mixtral-8x7B models with tensor parallelism. Higher is better.\nresult from KV cache reuse, the exploitation of parallelism within a single program, and faster\nconstrained decoding. Next, we explain the reasons for the speedup in each benchmark.\nOn MMLU, SGLang can reuse the KV cache of the 5-shot examples with RadixAttention. RadixAt-\ntention benefits both throughput and latency. RadixAttention reduces total memory usage by sharing\nthe KV cache, allowing for a larger batch size to improve maximum throughput. RadixAttention\nalso reduces the computation of prefill, thus decreasing the first token latency. On HellaSwag,\nSGLang reuses the KV cache of both few-shot examples and the common question prefix for multiple\nchoices, resulting in two-level sharing. For the ReAct and generative agents, SGLang reuses the\nKV cache of the agent template and previous calls. On Tree-of-thought and Skeleton-of-thought,\nSGLang parallelizes the generation calls within a single program and reuses the KV cache as much\nas possible. On JSON decoding, SGLang accelerates decoding by decoding multiple tokens at once\nwith a compressed finite state machine. In multi-turn chat, SGLang reuses the KV cache of the chat\nhistory. The speedup is more noticeable for short outputs because KV cache reuse mostly helps\nreduce the prefix time. For long outputs, because there is not much sharing between different chat\nsessions and the decoding time dominates, there is almost no speedup. In the DSPy RAG pipeline,\nSGLang reuses the KV cache of the common context example. On these benchmarks, the cache hit\nrate ranges from 50% to 99%. Fig. 13 (Appendix) lists the achieved and optimal cache hit rates for all\nof them, showing that our cache-aware scheduling approaches 96% of the optimal hit rate on average.\nWe exclude LMQL and Guidance from some of the last five benchmarks due to slow performance and\nmissing functionalities. LMQL’s issues stem from slow token-level processing and an unoptimized\nbackend, while Guidance lacks batching and parallelism support.\nResults on larger models with tensor parallelism. We run larger models, Mixtral-8x7B and Llama-\n70B, with tensor parallelism on the same set of benchmarks and report the results in Fig. 7 and Fig. 12\n(Appendix). The speedup on larger models shows a trend similar to that observed on smaller models,\nindicating that our optimization generalizes well to larger models. We omit Guidance and LMQL\nhere because they lack efficient implementations of tensor parallelism.\nResults on multi-modal models. SGLang has native support for multi-modal models with the\nimage and video primitives. The optimizations in this paper are compatible with multi-modal\nmodels. For RadixAttention, we compute the hash of the input images and use it as the key in the\nradix tree, allowing us to reuse the KV cache of the image tokens from the same image. We run\nLLaVA-v1.5-7B (image) on llava-bench-in-the-wild and LLaVA-NeXT-34B (video) on ActivityNet.\nBecause these models are not well supported by other baseline systems, we use the model authors’\noriginal implementation in Hugging Face Transformers as the baseline. As shown in Table 2, SGLang\nprovides throughput up to 6× higher on these benchmarks. In llava-bench-in-the-wild, there are\nmultiple questions about the same image, and SGLang runtime reuses the KV cache in this case.\nProduction deployment. SGLang has been deployed in Chatbot Arena [8] to serve open-weight\nmodels. Due to low traffic for some models, only one SGLang worker serves each. After one\nmonth, we observed a 52.4% RadixAttention cache hit rate for LLaVA-Next-34B [28] and 74.1% for\nVicuna-33B [7]. Cache hits come from common system messages, frequently reused example images,\nand multi-turn chat histories. This reduces first-token latency by an average of 1.7× for Vicuna-33B.\n8\n\n\n--- PAGE 9 ---\nTable 2: Throughput comparison on multi-modal LLaVA image and video models.\nModel\nLLaVA-v1.5-7B (image)\nLLaVA-NeXT-34B (video)\nAuthor’s original implementation\n0.18 image/s\n0.02 frame/s\nSGLang\n1.15 image/s\n0.10 frame/s\n0\n20\n40\n60\n80 100\nCache Hit Rate (%)\n20\n30\n40\n(a)\nBatch Size\nThroughput (tokens / s)\n0\n20\n40\n60\n80 100\nCache Hit Rate (%)\n200\n300\n400\n(b)\nTotal Latency (s)\nFirst Token Latency (s)\nLLM Judge\nTree of\nThought\nMMLU\nMulti-Turn\nChat(short)\n0.00\n0.25\n0.50\n0.75\n1.00\nThroughput\n(Normalized)\n(c)\nNo Cache\nNo Tree Structure\nFCFS Schedule\nRandom Schedule\nNo Frontend Parallelism\nNo Frontend Hint\nFull Optimization\n0.4k\n0.6k\n0.8k\n1.0k\n1.2k\n10\n20\nFigure 8: (a)(b) Cache hit rate ablation study. (c) RadixAttention ablation study.\nResults on API models. We test a prompt that extracts three fields from a Wikipedia page using\nOpenAI’s GPT-3.5 model. By using few-shot prompting, the accuracy of API speculative execution\nis high, and it reduces the cost of input tokens by about threefold due to the extraction of three fields.\n6.3\nAblation Study\nCache hit rate vs. latency/throughput. Fig. 8(a)(b) shows the relationship between cache hit\nrate and performance metrics (first token latency, total latency, batch size, and throughput) on the\ntree-of-thought benchmark. The figure is obtained by partially disabling matched tokens at runtime.\nIt shows that a higher cache hit rate leads to a larger batch size, higher throughput, and lower latency.\nEffectiveness of RadixAttention. We test the effectiveness of RadixAttention and its components\non several representative benchmarks. As shown in Fig. 8(c), \"No Cache\" means not using any\ncache, \"No Tree-Structure\" means using a simple table-based cache instead of a tree-structured cache,\n\"FCFS Schedule\" means using a first-come-first-serve policy instead of our cache-aware scheduling,\n\"Random Schedule\" means using a random order to schedule requests, \"No Frontend Parallelism\"\nmeans disabling parallelism in the interpreter, \"No Frontend Hint\" means disabling sending the\nfork hints from the interpreters, and \"Full optimizations\" means we turn on all optimizations. The\nexperimental results show that each of these components is required to achieve the best performance.\nDisabling parallelism and hints from the frontend interpreter also results in suboptimal runtime\nperformance, highlighting the importance of co-designing the frontend language and runtime.\nOverhead of RadixAttention. We test the overhead of RadixAttention on a benchmark without any\nKV cache reuse opportunities. The benchmark measures throughput on the ShareGPT dataset. It\ntakes 74.3 seconds to run 100 requests; however, the time used for managing the RadixAttention data\nstructures is only 0.2 seconds, which is a negligible overhead of less than 0.3%. This is because the\ncomplexity of tree operations is linear and small. Thus, we can turn on RadixAttention by default.\nEffectiveness of the compressed finite state machine. We test the effectiveness of the compressed\nfinite state machine and its components on the JSON decoding benchmark. Experimental results\nshow that the compressed finite state machine increases the throughput by 1.6× because it can decode\nmultiple tokens at once. In addition, we need to preprocess the state machine and reuse it for a batch\nof requests. Otherwise, redoing the preprocessing for each request makes the throughput 2.4× lower.\n7\nRelated Work\nVarious works have explored the reuse of the KV cache, and many of them are concurrent with our\nwork. Uniquely, our RadixAttention first proposes treating the KV cache as a tree-based LRU cache.\nIt is the first solution that supports multi-level sharing, cache-aware scheduling, frontend-runtime\nco-scheduling, and distributed cases. vLLM [23] and ChunkedAttention [58] explore some simple\nreuse cases (e.g., system prompt sharing) but do not cover multi-level tree-structured sharing or LRU\ncaching. PromptCache [12] proposes the modular reuse of the KV cache beyond the prefix but can\nimpact accuracy by up to a 43% drop. HydraGen [18], FlashInfer [59], and ChunkedAttention [58]\nfocus on CUDA kernel optimizations and do not include the concept of an LRU cache. API Serve [1]\nand LLM-SQL [29] study KV cache reuse for specific applications such as interleaving with external\nAPI calls and relational databases, but they do not have our radix tree or cache-aware scheduling.\n9\n\n\n--- PAGE 10 ---\nSeveral LLM programming and agent frameworks exist, such as Guidance [13], LMQL [4],\nDSPy [20], LangChain [24], AutoGen [55], and LLM Compiler [21]. Guidance and LMQL are most\nsimilar to SGLang, and we compare them in Sec. 2. Our innovation lies in novel runtime optimizations\nfor accelerating the proposed programming model. SGLang is compatible with other frameworks and\ncan accelerate them (e.g., the DSPy example in our evaluation). Additionally, SGLang is compatible\nwith many other common inference optimizations [60, 39, 3, 23, 59, 10, 26, 15, 19, 32, 31, 11].\n8\nFuture Directions and Conclusion\nFuture directions. Despite the progress made with SGLang, several limitations remain that reveal\npromising directions for future research. These include extending SGLang to support additional\noutput modalities, adapting RadixAttention to operate across multiple levels of the memory hierarchy\n(e.g., DRAM, Disk) [43], enabling fuzzy semantic matching within RadixAttention, providing higher-\nlevel primitives atop SGLang, fixing starvation in cache-aware scheduling [42], and enhancing the\nSGLang compiler to perform advanced static optimizations such as scheduling and memory planning.\nConclusion. We introduce SGLang, a framework for efficient programming and executing structured\nlanguage model programs. SGLang significantly improves the throughput and latency of complex\nLM programs through novel optimizations like RadixAttention, compressed finite state machines,\nand a language interpreter. It is a valuable tool for developing advanced prompting techniques and\nagent workflows. The source code is publicly available.\nAcknowledgement\nThis project is supported by the Stanford Center for Automated Reasoning and gifts from Astronomer,\nGoogle, IBM, Intel, Lacework, Microsoft, Mohamed Bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence,\nNexla, Samsung SDS, Uber, and VMware.
